Still, a slow, bubbling anger and resentment began to build, ultimately turning into an uprising that saw the Imperials ejected from their home planet. Their spirit rekindled, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") have learned not to be duped or cowed again, and most take the concept of freedom very seriously. [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") despise injustice, and most go out of their way to help weaker individuals or those suffering under the weight of oppression.

## History and Culture

Little is known about the history of the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren"), and most of the records that they keep are oral in tradition. Myths persist that [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") was once a lush planet, rich with water, plant life, and animals. [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") elders tell tales that untold eons ago, one of the "wandering ones who danced across the sky" grew jealous of Nizon's bounty and the decadence of the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") who took their utopia for granted. The "wanderer" grew bright in the sky and released a massive flash of light. Soon stones and fire rained down from the sky, which had grown black and filled with toxic fumes. The sun seemed to grow bigger in the sky, and the planet heated up.

Eventually [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") became the desolate, windswept rock that it is today.

Scholars have deduced from this myth that [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") received the brunt of a cataclysmic collision with a nearby planet and some other celestial body a half-million years ago or more. [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") was shifted closer to the sun, and the axial tilt went awry. The system shows signs of this disaster even today, with an immense asteroid belt that makes entering or leaving [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") a dicey proposition.

Ironically, this disaster may have been one of the Nazren's saving graces. Long ago, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") did indeed live in a virtual utopia, but corruption and decadence eventually led to the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") hunting each other or engaging in skirmishes and wars for sport. When the world's environment turned, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") splintered into small family groups in order to survive, and the idea of attacking their fellows become abhorrent. The [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") still kept weapons for hunting, but they became more peaceful as their attentions turned toward agriculture, grown in underground tunnels and caves beneath the rocky outcroppings and foothills that became their new bases of operation.

Prior to the Empire's arrival and their subsequent enslavement, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") lived a seminomadic lifestyle. Small family groups and larger clans traveled in slow, meandering routes, moving from rock outcropping to mountain hold as the seasons dictated. During the cooler winter months, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") picked up stakes and hit the dusty trail, moving in.

Although small outposts and meeting areas dot the planet, there is only one major city on the whole of [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon"). This settlement doesn't even have a proper name- the term Ahdjok, which means "city" in [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren"), is the only description it has, and the term is used primarily by the other sentient beings that have settled there after mining ventures were established on [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") and nearby [Centares](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Centares?action=edit&redlink=1 "Centares (page does not exist)").

Everything changed when the Empire arrived. Although other groups and [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") (mainly mining corporations and wildcatters) had arrived and settled on [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") beforehand, nothing prepared the natives for the upheaval to come. Imperial patrols began rounding up entire tribes of [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren"), starting with those in and around Ahdjok. Their interrogation of prisoners revealed the locations of other tribes and travel routes. Soon, a huge portion of the population was rounded up and brought back to Ahdjok for incarceration- or for shipment to [Centares](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Centares?action=edit&redlink=1 "Centares (page does not exist)") or beyond for use as slave labor.

## Politics

Prior to the arrival of the Empire, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") lacked much in the way of a planetwide government, with most decisions being made at a tribal level. Even then, few if any tribes relied on the voice of a single leader to make decisions, relying on the consensus of councils comprised of elders, heavily influenced by the individual members of the tribe. Everyone has a say in important matters, meaning that many decisions move at a glacial pace. However, as mentioned above, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are an extremely patient Species, and they are confident when a decision is finally reached and accept it without further debate.

Once every two years, tribes gather at a predetermined spot to work out trade deals and discuss concerns that affect the entire population. Ahdjok served as one of these meeting places, although many other places exist around the planet. However, Ahdjok did serve the purpose of hosting one of the most sacred and revered places on the planet: Martyr's Plaza. This 9-meter-wide dome is crafted of stone and topped by a large hatch. The walls of this dome are covered with bas relief and scrollwork, detailing the deeds, history, and heroics of the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") people.

Three huge statues stand around the dome; ancient and weathered, these robed figures hold their arms to the sky. Strangely, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are relatively unconcerned with maintaining these statues, and they seem to totter on their crumbling bases. One of the statues, the great prophet [Sulkot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sulkot?action=edit&redlink=1 "Sulkot (page does not exist)"), leans precariously, even swaying slightly in the ever-howling winds.

Beyond its spiritual importance, Martyr's Plaza also serves another purpose- the planet's primary communication center sits beneath the dome. Old but hardy computers in this communication center plot the asteroids and meteorites that swarm the skies above [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon"). Without these computers, navigation in and out of the planet's atmosphere would prove exceedingly difficult, and the recapturing of this place helped the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") to overthrow the Empire and keep them from returning.

Another vital location is Mount Antas, which sits immediately adjacent to Ahdjok. The mountain serves the main purpose of shielding the city from the worst of the wind and dust that would otherwise engulf the settlement. When the Empire took over the city, they began building a [Turbolaser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolaser "Turbolaser") battery and shield generator to help them defend the planet from would-be attackers. When the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") overthrew the Imperials, they continued the Empire's plans and finished the projects, ensuring the safety of the planet for the future.

The [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren"), along with the help of other sentients, have begun to hollow out Mount Antas to build redundant navigation and guidance computers and to help improve the infrastructure of the planet. As more Nazren flock from the deserts to help in these projects, important skills are learned and more trade flows toward [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon").

After the rebellion, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") elected a "mayor" of Ahdjok, who also serves as a sort of planetary governor. The [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are learning the art and science of politics, trade deals, and self-governance in ways that their ancestors never would have imagined. The relatively few [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") found outside of [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") do not have any meaningful galactic representation, sticking to tribal laws and hierarchy among themselves.

## Technology

[Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") is a technological backwater, and most [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are ignorant of many of the wonders available in the rest of the galaxy. Only the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") who have been to Ahdjok know of the bewildering array of [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species"), weapons, [Starships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships "Starships"), and other technological marvels that exist. In fact, some [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") who live in the deepest portions of the desert or in mountain warrens still think that their Species is the only intelligent one in all the galaxy.

Most of the native technology on the planet is geared toward survival on such an arid, windswept planet. The [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") have created fine quality [Breath Masks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Breath_Masks "Breath Masks") that last considerably longer than those brought in from the outside. Most natives don't need the masks; they are made primarily for the elderly and those with respiratory problems. Moisture vaporators are also common sites at the various way stations of [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon"), especially around Ahdjok and up the sides of Mount Antas.

After the overthrow of the Empire on [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon"), the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") scavenged whatever technology, weapons, and ships they could and began learning the Imperials' secrets. Over time, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") proved capable of building their own repulsorlift and other vital technologies. While still far behind the galactic technological curve, [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") is slowly improving its status.

## Trade

Prior to the Empire's arrival, [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") had little to offer to the galaxy, and trade was a limited affair. Nearby [Centares](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Centares?action=edit&redlink=1 "Centares (page does not exist)") has a considerably more advanced technological level. [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") does have its share of valuable minerals and metals and has allowed a small number of mining companies to exploit these resources, primarily in areas that the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") otherwise avoid or deem unimportant.

Within their own culture, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") have a complex system of trading that primarily focuses around food, water, and materials to make shelters and clothing. Today, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are hungry for more advanced technology and are willing to make bold trade agreements to get what they need, up to and including leasing huge portions of land or using their main asset- themselves- in deals to bolster trade. However, unlike in the days of Imperial occupation, the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") are wary of exploitation and demand a handsome price for their services as laborers.

## Nazren in the Galaxy

Due to its obscure location and the fact that its home system is filled with dangerous asteroids, [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon") is practically unknown by the rest of the galaxy. When other sentients arrived on [Nizon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nizon "Nizon"), some of the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") left their planet to see what was beyond their homeworld. When the Empire came and enslaved the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren"), the slaves were sent throughout the galaxy to work in mines and serve as grunt labor. Although many of these [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") remain in captivity even with the freedom of their home planet, some have managed to escape and can be found almost anywhere.

With their limited numbers and lack of representation within the galactic community, most [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") do what they can to survive. The natural strength and hardy nature of the [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") mean that most find work as bodyguards, laborers, and the like, though the necessities of survival mean that [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") could be found doing almost any sort of occupation. Their nomadic nature also means that [Nazren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nazren "Nazren") keep on the move and find [Starship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship "Starship") travel a fantastic way to soothe their wanderlust.

Over the course of galactic history, certain items, including weapons and armor, have been made famous by the person they belong to. The personal [Blaster Rifle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blaster_Rifle "Blaster Rifle") of a hero of [The Mandalorian Wars](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Mandalorian_Wars "The Mandalorian Wars") or a [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") wielded by a dreaded [Sith Lord](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Lord "Sith Lord") is more than just another piece of [Equipment](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Equipment "Equipment"); it is a piece of history. It has become legendary because it is strongly associated with the power and destiny of its owner and is closely tied to that person's successes; for example, Ulic Qel-Droma's [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") and Exar Kun's [Battle Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Armor "Battle Armor") were both integral in helping those characters fulfill their [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"). Heirloom Items are exceedingly rare, and each is unique. Thus, they are highly sought-after by collectors and heroes alike.

The original owners of Heirloom Items take exceptional care of those items and might have specific talent or skill in maintaining and upgrading them. An Heirloom Item carries a benefit that persists until the item's destruction. Such a benefit makes the item special and is the result of the original owner's tinkering or a side effect of being present when a character's [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny") is fulfilled. See the section below for several sample heirloom item properties.

## Creating Heirloom Items

In order for an Heirloom Item to come into being, the item must be present when a character fulfills his or her [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"). When a character fulfills his or her [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"), instead of gaining the "Destiny Fulfilled" effect of that [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"), he or she can instead choose to create an Heirloom Item. That character then chooses one of the following effects, which is applied to a particular weapon, suit of armor, or item in the character's possession at the moment the [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny") is fulfilled. Typically this item is one of particular significance to the character.

Additionally, when a character with an unfulfilled destiny dies while in pursuit of his or her [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"), instead of the normal effect ([Force Spirit](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Spirit "Force Spirit"), [Noble Sacrifice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble_Sacrifice "Noble Sacrifice"), or [Vengeance](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vengeance "Vengeance")) that character can choose to create an Heirloom Item instead. The possible Heirloom Item effects are discussed below:

- **Extra Modification:** The owner can modify the item a second time using the [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist") Feat.
- **Legendary Icon:** When carrying in plain sight, the owner gains favorable circumstances on all [Charisma](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charisma "Charisma")-based [Skill Checks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Checks "Skill Checks") (except [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") checks).
- **Steeped in the Dark Side:** Once per encounter, while carrying or using the Heirloom Item, the owner can apply a [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny") bonus equal to one-half their [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") on any attack roll or [Skill Check](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Check "Skill Check") made before the end of their turn. Applying this bonus increases the owner's [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") by 1.
- **Symbol of the Light:** When carrying or using the Heirloom Item, once per encounter, as a [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction"), the owner can impose a penalty on any one attack roll or [Skill Check](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Check "Skill Check") made by a character with a [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") of 1 or higher. This penalty is equal to the target's [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score").

## Behind the Threat: The Sith

Part 1: PortrayalPart 2: The BecomingPart 3: DesignPart 4: The Teeth in the DarknessPart 5: Antiheroes

*By Matthew Grau*

[The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"). The very name has haunted [The Jedi Order](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi_Order "The Jedi Order") for millennia. This cult of Force practitioners has known both absolute power and unconscionable self-destruction. Indeed, they are something of an enigma- nightmares to all, but to themselves, a natural expression of [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force").

It is no surprise that many players are drawn to the idea of exploring [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") and creating Sith characters. Although *[Threats of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threats_of_the_Galaxy "Threats of the Galaxy")* examines [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") as enemies, this series of articles will explore the different aspects involved in developing, portraying, and using compelling and unique Sith characters in five eras of the *Star Wars* saga.

## The Sith Code

*Peace is a lie, there is only passion.*

*Through passion, I gain strength.*

*Through strength, I gain power.*

*Through power, I gain victory.*

*Through victory, my chains are broken.*

*The Force shall free me.*

*-From the Sith Code*

[The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") are creatures of passion and lust. [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") has its allure because it calls to the things we crave most in life, seducing those whose desires outstrip their sanity.

Selflessness is not a concept inherent in the Sith way of being. Those who choose to serve [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") out of devotion to a philosophical or religious ideal are rare. The exploration of passion inevitably leads to desire, and delving deeply into desire inevitably leads to greed. Power corrupts, and [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") are no exception. Though one may begin on the path intending balance or even commanding [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") for altruistic purposes, all fall prey to greed and vanity. This is why groups of Sith, left to their own devices, eventually turn on each other. They are predators, and sooner or later, Sith regard their brethren as threats.

You might say the key difference between [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi") and [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") is that the Jedi contemplate and master inner space to serve others, whereas the Sith dominate and master outer space to serve themselves.

## Eras of the Sith

[The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") figure into five important eras of the *Star Wars* saga. The area you choose in which to play greatly determines the kind of Sith you'll be designing and how they fit in with the galaxy. It's important that you choose an era that works with the kind of Sith game you'd like to play.

### [The Old Republic Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Old_Republic_Era "The Old Republic Era")

Four thousand years before [The Galactic Empire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Galactic_Empire "The Galactic Empire"), [Darth Revan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Revan "Darth Revan") brought [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") into the light after visiting Sith worlds and basking in the power of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). [Revan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Revan "Revan") and their apprentice, [Darth Malak](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Malak "Darth Malak"), brought about the bloody Jedi Civil War. They established the new Sith Empire and nearly wiped out [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi").

Since [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") are prevalent and operate openly in this era, it is easy to play games involving Sith characters.

### [The Rise of the Empire Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Rise_of_the_Empire_Era "The Rise of the Empire Era")

The Rule of Two characterized this era, as [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") completed his apprenticeship under the tutelage of [Darth Plagueis](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Plagueis?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Plagueis (page does not exist)") the Wise, and killed his master while he slept. Sidious first took [Darth Maul](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Maul "Darth Maul") as his apprentice, and after the vicious [Zabrak](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Zabrak "Zabrak") was slain by [Obi-Wan Kenobi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Obi-Wan_Kenobi "Obi-Wan Kenobi"), [Darth Tyranus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Tyranus "Darth Tyranus") slipped under his wing. Tyranus was, in turn, killed by [Anakin Skywalker](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Anakin_Skywalker "Anakin Skywalker"), who took Tyranus' place and became one of the most dreaded Lords of the Sith, [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader").

During this era, Darth Sidious' schemes required obfuscation and no lesser amount of treachery. His plans hinged on The Jedi's belief that [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") were extinct. Although Sidious may have sheltered and dispatched Dark Jedi to satisfy his plans, he would not train them in the ways of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith").

Under canon, it is difficult to justify characters during this era who are true Sith. Dark Jedi characters, on the other hand, fit easily into the growing Sith menace. However, you could create a splinter sect of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") that has remained hidden in other parts of the galaxy, like that of [Darth Millennial](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Millennial?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Millennial (page does not exist)") and his Dark Force religion.

### [The Rebellion Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Rebellion_Era "The Rebellion Era")

After [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") created [The Galactic Empire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Galactic_Empire "The Galactic Empire"), dissolved the Senate, and usurped control of the galaxy, he eased the Rule of Two a bit. He created the Secret Order of the Empire and brought Dark Side Force practitioners into the fold to be his prophets. He and Vader trained many [Dark Side Adepts](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Adepts_(CL_6) "Dark Side Adepts (CL 6)"), using Dark Jedi as their most trusted and dangerous of servants, including the Emperor's Hand. However, the Rule of Two is kept intact in its strictest sense as neither Sidious or Vader passed on the true legacy and ways of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith").

As in [The Rise of the Empire Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Rise_of_the_Empire_Era "The Rise of the Empire Era"), true Sith are rare. Dark Jedi, however, are even more prevalent and can operate openly in the service of the Emperor. Again, other Sith with different views might exist in the shadows of the galaxy, but they would have to remain concealed. Most likely, [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") would view them as a threat to be extinguished (an interesting plot device in its own right). However, [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader") did have at least one secret apprentice...

### [The New Jedi Order Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_New_Jedi_Order_Era "The New Jedi Order Era")

Although the Rule of Two prevented Vader from taking on a true apprentice, he did pass his teachings to pupils, notably the Dark Lady of the Sith, [Lumiya](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lumiya "Lumiya"). She trained several apprentices during this era, but they are not truly Sith because [Lumiya](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lumiya "Lumiya") herself does not become full-fledged Sith until her training by [Vergere](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vergere?action=edit&redlink=1 "Vergere (page does not exist)").

In [The New Jedi Order Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_New_Jedi_Order_Era "The New Jedi Order Era"), [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") are all but extinct. It is only through the training of [Vergere](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vergere?action=edit&redlink=1 "Vergere (page does not exist)") that [Lumiya](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lumiya "Lumiya") becomes a true Dark Lady of the Sith. Dark Jedi characters would be more appropriate.

### [The Legacy Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Legacy_Era "The Legacy Era")

During [The Confederation-Galactic Alliance War](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Confederation-Galactic_Alliance_War?action=edit&redlink=1 "The Confederation-Galactic Alliance War (page does not exist)"), the Dark Lady [Lumiya](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lumiya "Lumiya") resurrected the Rule of Two and found herself the perfect apprentice- the grandson of [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader"). [Jacen Solo](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jacen_Solo?action=edit&redlink=1 "Jacen Solo (page does not exist)") followed in his ancestor's footsteps and became [Darth Caedus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Caedus?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Caedus (page does not exist)").

While Caedus was busy trying to gather power, he drew all the attention of [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi") to him. Meanwhile, hidden on the ancient Sith planet [Korriban](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Korriban "Korriban"), [Darth Krayt](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Krayt "Darth Krayt") gathered other Dark Jedi to his cause and once again founded an Order of the Sith. This time, however, it was based on Krayt's Rule of One- there would be only one Order, under which there would be many Sith. Eventually, [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") returned in numbers and brought the galaxy to its knees.

During the time of [Darth Caedus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Caedus?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Caedus (page does not exist)"), Sith are even more rare than they were in [The Rebellion Era](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Rebellion_Era "The Rebellion Era"). Again, Dark Jedi would be more appropriate as characters. However, after [Darth Krayt](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Krayt "Darth Krayt") emerges with his new Sith Order, [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") once again become plentiful in the galaxy, more so than in any other era but those of the ancient past.

## Crafting the Sith

To create a compelling Sith character, you must consider several important factors, including their reason for falling to [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"), their personal interpretation of Sith beliefs, and their torturous apprenticeship.

### Falling to the Dark Side

Though each Sith is unique, they share things in common. The most obvious is the presence of a person or event that pulled them to [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). Many fall in pursuit of power; their desire for personal gain draws them to a philosophy of absolutes. Many others fall prey through their inability to resolve deep hurt, anger, fear, or hatred. These emotions consume their sanity and draw them into a spiral of suffering, transforming them into a potential Sith.

But these are not the only reasons. Some begin with a desire to discover balance in [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force"), believing that a true understanding of existence is possible only by examining the Light and Dark facets of all beings. Others begin with an altruistic desire to help others by rapidly increasing their power or gaining access to mythical abilities that only [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") can offer. Some are drawn through a misguided hunger for curiosity, a deep desire simply to know. Most often, it is a combination of these factors, but they all lead down the same path.

This crucial event is the foundation in Sith character design. It helps determine who they are on a core level, as well as what might redeem them (if the opportunity arises).

It might be helpful to sketch out a basic story of how your character first encountered the power of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). What made them curious enough to play with fire and ultimately become baptized by it, reborn as a creature of darkness?

While the most obvious class progression is from the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") Heroic Class to the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class (and eventually to the [Sith Lord](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Lord "Sith Lord") Prestige Class), there are many other ways of arriving that might better fit your character's background. For example, [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") was never officially trained as a [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi"), instead spreading his web of intrigue as a [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble"). The greatest restriction to entering the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class is learning [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as a [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill"), but there are several methods to obtaining said [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill").

As long as you have [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity"), you can train in [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") by any of the traditional skill training methods, including the [Skill Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Training "Skill Training") Feat, increasing your [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") to the point where you get a new [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill"), or taking [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") at 1st level and spending one of your starting [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") on [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force").

Another method is, of course, taking levels in the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") Heroic Class, the only [Heroic Class](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heroic_Class "Heroic Class") that has [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") as a starting Feat, which can also be taken as a bonus Feat when [Multiclassing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Multiclassing "Multiclassing") into [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi"). Remember that levels in the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") class do not necessarily mean training in [The Jedi Order](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi_Order "The Jedi Order"). Instead, they could indicate training from a [Force Tradition](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Tradition "Force Tradition") or simply the first steps of training by a Sith Master.

A character must have seven levels (in any combination of [Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Classes "Classes")) to be eligible for the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class.

### Beliefs

The philosophy of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") is unique. It is a cult dedicated to the most dangerous of emotions, yet it rises to galaxy-shaking power again and again. One of the primary reasons for this is The Sith's absolute devotion to the law of the jungle- only the strong survive, and it is the right of the strong to prey on the weak. Through perpetual conflict, the strong are pushed down the path of perfection. This is the reason [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") have pressed the galaxy into war and oppression time and again, some for reasons of personal strength and gain, and others through a philosophy of forcing change and evolution. Morality and peace are only obstructions to this ultimate goal.

[The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") also believe that the path to power is to attain a more holistic view of [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") by embracing their passions. Whereas [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi") seek to overcome "negative" emotions such as pain, anger, and hate, [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") welcome them as natural- and as fuel in their quest for power.

These are the fundamental beliefs of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"), but each one internalizes and acts on them differently. Take a moment to determine which of these beliefs are most important to your character and how they work to enforce them on others and on the galaxy as a whole.

### Apprenticeship

When developing a Sith character, consider their schooling in [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). How did they slip under the wing of their master? What was their apprenticeship like? Every Sith Apprentice stirs in the shadows of their master for many years as they are instructed in [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). The nature of this apprenticeship varies, but it relies on cruelty and manipulation. The lessons are designed to nurture the consuming passions of the apprentice while cultivating things that lead to aggression, such as pain, malice, and revenge.

In addition, Sith training works to eradicate fear and compassion- two emotions that cripple the strong. That which a Sith Lord fears or finds compassion for has power over them, something which they must not abide. Thus, they are forced to confront and conquer their fears, usually in traumatic ways, and the things they love are stolen from them, one by one, until they are no longer tethered to anything but their devotion to [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith").

Apprenticeship is extreme, requiring tireless years of training. During the period when the Rule of Two prevailed, one Sith Apprentice must be more than a match for several Jedi since [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") do not have the luxury of numbers. Like most great predators, a single Sith Lord, even an apprentice, is something to be feared.

Most apprenticeships conclude the day the student is willing and able to commit the ultimate act of "selflessness" and devotion to [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith")- killing or destroying someone that they love. This action demonstrates their embodiment of unattached strength, power, and mercilessness. For example, [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader") sacrificed [Mace Windu](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mace_Windu "Mace Windu") and, ultimately, [The Jedi Order](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi_Order "The Jedi Order"). [Darth Caedus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Caedus?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Caedus (page does not exist)") murdered his aunt, [Mara Jade Skywalker](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mara_Jade_Skywalker "Mara Jade Skywalker"). Once this act is complete, the apprentice may take on the title of Sith Lord and their new name.

Nothing is more formative in a Sith's life than their apprenticeship. When they become a Sith Lord, it is the Sith Lord that their master crafted them to be.

If you haven't already done so, consider who your character's master was and how they trained your character. What were their harshest lessons? What did your character have to do to conquer their fears? Who was the beloved person in your character's life that they murdered to finally join the ranks of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith")?

In addition, apprenticeship is the time when a Sith begins to hone and direct his abilities. What are your character's first [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents")? Do they come from the [Armor Specialist Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor_Specialist_Talent_Tree "Armor Specialist Talent Tree"), reflecting a Sith who appreciates a striking suit of armor (such as [Darth Revan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Revan "Darth Revan") or [Darth Krayt](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Krayt "Darth Krayt"))? Do they come from the [Duelist Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Duelist_Talent_Tree "Duelist Talent Tree") or [Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber_Combat_Talent_Tree "Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree"), reflecting a Sith who appreciates the elegance of their [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") (such as [Darth Tyranus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Tyranus "Darth Tyranus") or [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader"))? Do they from the [Alter Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Alter_Talent_Tree "Alter Talent Tree"), [Control Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Control_Talent_Tree "Control Talent Tree"), or [Sense Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sense_Talent_Tree "Sense Talent Tree"), reflecting a Sith who deeply connects with the power of [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") (again, such as [Darth Tyranus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Tyranus "Darth Tyranus") or [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader")). Do they come from the [Dark Side Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Talent_Tree "Dark Side Talent Tree") or [Sith Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Talent_Tree "Sith Talent Tree"), reflecting an apprentice who strongly embraces the power of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") (such as [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious"))?

*By Matthew Grau*

To symbolize the giving up of their old life, a Sith Apprentice takes on a new name. In all eras after [Darth Bane](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Bane "Darth Bane"), [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") take on the honorific Darth, a title that caused jealousy and infighting in ancient times. Darth is often thought to be a contraction of Dark Lord of the Sith, the first recorded use of which was in the ancient times of [Darth Revan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Revan "Darth Revan") and [Darth Malak](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Malak "Darth Malak"). Some believe it derives from the [Rakata](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rakata "Rakata"), an ancient race of corrupt creatures whose horrifying Infinite Empire was a tool of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"), possibly meaning "emperor" or "immortal." The name given to a Sith Lord by their master is often something associated with the dark passions he or she revels. Bane, Krayt (as in the terrifying dragons of [Tatooine](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tatooine "Tatooine")), Maul, Plagueis (as in plague), Sidious (as in insidious), and Tyranus (as in tyranny) are examples of things that appeal to [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith").

In the days of the Rule of Two, the relationship of Apprentice and Master can end only one way- with the death of the Master. Few apprentices are content to wait for their master to die of natural causes to take on the mantle of power, and, as in the Sith legend of [Darth Plagueis](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Plagueis?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Plagueis (page does not exist)") the Wise, many Masters are killed by their apprentices once they have taught everything they know. Sith Masters are hardly fools and know that death at the hands of their pupils is a very real possibility, but they view this as a necessary danger to keep the Sith lineage strong.

Now is the time to create your character's Sith name. Most likely you'll be using the honorific Darth, but what sort of dark, poetic name did your character's Master gift them with? The examples here should provide a good baseline from which to work.

It is also time to determine the current state of your character's relationship with their Master. Are they still in your character's life, or are they dead? If they're in your character's life, what is their relationship? How do they guide and use your character as their apprentice? If they're dead, did they die by incident, or did your character kill them to seize their power?

All Sith share a deep hatred of [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi"). They view the Light Side Order as their ancient enemies, the one force that has stood in their way time and time again and the one thing capable of defeating them. [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") look forward to a day when they can truly wipe [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi") from the galaxy once and for all, something [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") and [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader") thought they had accomplished.

Contemplate your character's attitude toward [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi"). Do they actively seek to destroy them, or do they follow other pursuits (while remaining happy to rain down suffering on any Jedi who cross their path)?

## General Appearance

Typically, [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") have a distinctive appearance. Because of their obsessions and their views, they most often choose to stand out. Whether it be in their style of dress (such as the common choice of intimidating black robes or battle dress), in their personal affectation (such as wearing the bones of their victims or simply a wild style of hair or makeup), or just in the way they comport themselves, [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") do not blend in.

Then there are the eyes. The flooding power of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") often changes the eyes of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") to a sickly yellow, sometimes rimmed with red. The more Dark Side power that a Sith wields, the more likely it is that the change to their eyes is permanent. The eyes of a Sith are unnerving and can become difficult to conceal.

Many Sith Lords tattoo themselves in accordance with ancient custom, with black or alternating red and black markings covering their entire body. Some choose to decorate only part of their body, but since the tattoos are a symbol of their devotion to the order, the markings must be visible to all. Therefore, facial tattoos are common. Tattooing according to Sith tradition is, unsurprisingly, a very painful process.

Those forced to hide in plain sight are the exception, pretending to be as normal, upstanding, and even kindly as possible (such as [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") or [Darth Tyranus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Tyranus "Darth Tyranus")). Of course, their true nature stirs unseen, always plotting. A Dark Side practitioner may relish in the shadow play, building their lies and illusions, until it reaches just the right pitch to drop the pretense and reveal his allegiance. Ultimately, Sith do not prefer to hide what they are.

There are many facets to creating a unique look for your Sith character. The first question to answer: do they choose to fit in, or have they fully embraced the ways of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith")? If they choose to fit in, they will look just like everyone else most of the time. But like most Sith, they will have another face that they wear when among their own kind.

### Six Categories

The next question to consider: how does your character stand out? Though unique, most Sith fall into one of six categories:

1. Some Sith inspire fear from inside a suit of dreadful armor, such as the faceless [Darth Revan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Revan "Darth Revan") or the [Yuuzhan Vong](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Yuuzhan_Vong "Yuuzhan Vong") encasement of [Darth Krayt](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Krayt "Darth Krayt"). Though they are certainly awe-inspiring on their own, the armor of these Sith is a recognizable symbol of their might.
2. Some Sith (such as [Darth Maul](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Maul "Darth Maul") or [Darth Talon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Talon "Darth Talon")) undergo the painful process of extensive Sith tattooing, wearing the markings as a badge of pride to inspire terror. Although few who encounter the Sith may remember the details of their face or manner of dress, the tattoos will forever be burned in their minds.
3. Some choose to (or are forced to) embrace a deathlike visage, such as [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious") or [Darth Nihl](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Nihl "Darth Nihl"). Nearly every culture has a taboo about death, and most maintain some fear of it, so these Sith are instantly associated with that fear.
4. Some Sith choose to (or are forced to) embrace the otherworldly in their appearance, such as the ghostly [Darth Nihilus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Nihilus "Darth Nihilus") or the undead [Darth Sion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sion "Darth Sion"). Many people fear the unknown, and stories of ghosts and monsters are carried in cultures across the galaxy- a fact that these Sith prey upon.
5. Some (such as [Darth Caedus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Caedus?action=edit&redlink=1 "Darth Caedus (page does not exist)") or [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader") before his defeat on [Mustafar](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mustafar "Mustafar")) choose simply to let the power and bearing of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") ooze from their every action and are marked only by sickly yellow eyes. Their predatory movements and ways are typically enough to inspire awe and dread in those who encounter them.
6. The appearance of some Sith is a function of tragedy, such as the cybernetic [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader") or the undead [Darth Sion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sion "Darth Sion"). Vader was nearly killed on [Mustafar](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mustafar "Mustafar") by [Obi-Wan Kenobi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Obi-Wan_Kenobi "Obi-Wan Kenobi") and is sustained by extensive cybernetic augmentation; his frightening appearance is a function of his life-support system. Sion's body is broken, and he should be dead, but his hatred and powerful connection to [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") keep his body intact. [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") of this category wear their wounds with pride, boasting that they have cheated death.

These categories are an excellent place to start. Once you have determined the basic thrust of your character's Sith appearance, you can play with the particulars and even mix and match.

However, as they say, the devil is in the details. What kind of clothes do they wear? In what state are their eyes? Do they sometimes appear normal, or has the power of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") taken them for all time? If they have chosen to undergo the painful process of tattooing, what do the markings look like, and how do they display them? Do they have any unusual eccentricities in manner or habit? These questions are merely a start, limited only by your imagination.

As inspiration, you might wish to take a look at the famous Sith found in *[Threats of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threats_of_the_Galaxy "Threats of the Galaxy")*: [Darth Bane](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Bane "Darth Bane"), [Darth Maul](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Maul "Darth Maul"), and the Dark Lady [Lumiya](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lumiya "Lumiya").

| FEAT | PREREQUISITES | BENEFITS |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Dreadful Countenance](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dreadful_Countenance "Dreadful Countenance") | [Charisma](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charisma "Charisma") 13, Member of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") | Marked as Sith, your appearance inspires awe, dread, and fear in all who view it. |

*By Matthew Grau*

Not all Sith are fallen Jedi. There are as many paths to darkness as there are paths in life. One of the most infamous and powerful Sith was [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious"), a man named Palpatine who was a noble and a politician (growing out of the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") Heroic Class). Some were warriors or survivors of one sort or another, whose prowess and/or [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") drew the attention of a Sith Lord (growing out of the [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") or [Scout](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scout "Scout") Heroic Classes). Those who live in the shadows of society may already be inclined to the obsessions of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") simply due to the nature of their lifestyle, especially if they are Force-sensitive (growing out of the [Scoundrel](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scoundrel "Scoundrel") Heroic Class).

Others are [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") who fell or those whose control of [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") was developed outside [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi") tradition. [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader"), the scourge of [The Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi "The Jedi"), was in fact a powerful and respected one himself until the lure of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") claimed him. Perhaps the easiest explanation for a Force-using character to have strayed down the path of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") is that he lived in a world where falling to [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side") was a regular concern.

## Meeting the Requirements

The first threshold on the path of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") is to gain access to the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class. To do so, your character must be at least 7th level, have [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as a [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill"), and have the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") and [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)") Feats. They must also have acquired a [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") equal to their [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") and be a member of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"), but these are both accomplished through roleplaying. The [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat (which provides access to the [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") Skill) and the [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)") Feat are available only as starting [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") to the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") Heroic Class.

An easy way to speed you along your path is to have your character acquire at least one level in the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") class. Remember that this Jedi training does not have to be as a part of the Order; it can be from a variety of [Force Traditions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Traditions "Force Traditions") similar to [The Jedi Order](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Jedi_Order "The Jedi Order") or simply be the first step of training by a Sith Master.

Don't want your character to have been a [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") or to have had [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi")-like training? You can meet the requirements using your nonclass dependent [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), which are gained at 1st level, 3rd level, 6th level, and every three levels thereafter. At 1st level, by purchasing the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat, you are allowed to treat [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as a [Class Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Class_Skill "Class Skill"), which you can then choose as one of your starting [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills"). Later, choose [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)"), and you now have a character eligible for the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class, regardless of their background.

If your character is [Human](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Human "Human"), it's even easier because they receive one bonus [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat") and one bonus [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill") at creation. For example, you can choose [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") and [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)") as your character's starting [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") and make [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") your bonus [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill") (which they have access to through the former Feat).

The simplest option is to simply declare your character's first class to be [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi"), in which case you gain everything you need to eventually become Sith.

To develop [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers") during this time, your character needs to meet two of the same requirements- having the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat and being [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") in [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force"). Once you meet those requirements, you can select the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat. This Feat is not offered as a bonus Feat in any Heroic Class, so the only way to get it is through the [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") your character acquires as a result of Character Level, not Class Level.

### Choosing a Class

Once you figure out how to meet the requirements for the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class, you have room to play. You have at least six, if not seven, levels.

- If you wish your Sith to be manipulative and deceitful, the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") class is the way to go. Starting in the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") Heroic Class nets you more [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") than any other Heroic Class, among which are [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception"), [Gather Information](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gather_Information "Gather Information"), and [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion").
- Starting in the [Scout](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scout "Scout") Heroic Class nets you the second largest number of [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills"), among which are those that make a fiercely independent Sith, such as [Climb](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Climb "Climb"), [Endurance](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Endurance "Endurance"), [Jump](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jump "Jump"), [Survival](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Survival "Survival"), and [Swim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swim "Swim"), as well as the [Shake It Off](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shake_It_Off "Shake It Off") Feat.
- The [Scoundrel](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scoundrel "Scoundrel") Heroic Class, as a starting class, nets you the manipulative ways of the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") (though with fewer [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills")), adding the benefits of [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") and [Stealth](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stealth "Stealth") for those Sith wishing to be shadow warriors.
- For those on the path of a deadly predator, starting in the [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") Heroic Class is the way to go. Though your character won't have many [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills"), they will be geared toward aspects of combat, and the sheer number of starting weapon proficiencies makes up for it.

These guidelines are appropriate for choosing the Heroic Class in which to begin your character's path towards [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"). Once you begin to [Multiclass](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Multiclass "Multiclass"), it becomes more about the [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") and [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"). We'll discuss those more in Possible Paths, below.

### Choosing a Species

Some [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") are predisposed to [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") or have natural advantages that lend themselves well to the darker path.

- [Bothans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bothans "Bothans") can make dangerous Sith, but only when the Rule of Two is in place. Their manipulative and crafty natures make them vicious in a world of stealth and guile. If they have [Gather Information](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gather_Information "Gather Information") as a [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill"), they gain [Skill Focus (Gather Information)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Focus_(Gather_Information) "Skill Focus (Gather Information)") as a bonus Feat, and they have access to [The Bothan SpyNet](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Bothan_SpyNet "The Bothan SpyNet").
- Some [Quarren](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Quarren "Quarren") who endured the devastation of their homeworld during the Galactic Civil War live with so much pain and hate that they might be drawn naturally to the ways of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side").
- Natural hunters, Force-sensitive [Rodians](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rodians "Rodians") can find their aggression and tenacity bringing them toward the world of a Sith Warrior. Their Heightened Awareness and Low-Light Vision can be of great benefit to the [Rodian](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rodian "Rodian") Sith Warrior, giving them the ability to notice danger before many others and to strike more effectively under cover of darkness. However, they are poor deceivers (thanks to their -2 [Charisma](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charisma "Charisma")) and are better suited for more violent pursuits.
- [Sullustans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sullustans "Sullustans") are a race whose curiosity can easily lead to recklessness, and it is those same traits that can bring about the downfall of the [Sullustan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sullustan "Sullustan") Jedi who finds themselves fascinated by the ways of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"). Like [Rodians](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rodians "Rodians"), their Darkvision and Heightened Awareness can be an advantage for the Sith Warrior, giving them the ability to notice danger in advance and even more utility for striking from the shadows.
- The violent and driven nature of the [Trandoshans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trandoshans "Trandoshans") lends itself well to the path of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"), as do their natural combative abilities. They make deadly Sith Warriors, with their Darkvision, Limb Regeneration, and Natural Armor abilities, plus the bonus [Toughness](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Toughness "Toughness") Feat. They can take more punishment than most.
- Though [Twi'leks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Twi%27leks "Twi'leks") often try to avoid open conflict, their tendency to live in the shadows can bring those who master [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") to the stealth and guile of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") who live in the ages of the Rule of Two. Their +2 [Charisma](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charisma "Charisma") and Deceptive ability make them masters of manipulation.
- The adventurous nature of the [Zabrak](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Zabrak "Zabrak") likewise can be drawn to explore the mysteries of [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side"), and their adaptation to living in a tough environment serves them well as Sith Warriors (as evidenced by [Darth Maul](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Maul "Darth Maul")). Their Heightened Awareness and Superior Defenses abilities are of particular use.
- Still, [Humans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Humans "Humans") make up the bulk of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith"). The natural chaos of their emotions is capable of producing the warping passion necessary to create a Sith Lord. Many of the most powerful, terrifying, and notorious Sith Lords through history have been [Human](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Human "Human") or [Near-Human](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Near-Human "Near-Human"), including [Exar Kun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Exar_Kun "Exar Kun"), [Darth Malak](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Malak "Darth Malak"), [Darth Bane](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Bane "Darth Bane"), [Darth Sidious](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Sidious "Darth Sidious"), [Darth Vader](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Vader "Darth Vader"), and [Darth Krayt](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darth_Krayt "Darth Krayt"). Furthermore, their bonus [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat") and bonus [Trained Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skill "Trained Skill") give them more room to play when developing on their way to [The Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Dark_Side "The Dark Side").

## Possible Paths

Although there are many ways to craft a Sith, this section covers three main paths: the Sith Manipulator, the Sith Warrior, and the Fallen Jedi. Let's walk through the start of each kind of character through the final step of becoming a [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") at 7th level in terms of [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills"), [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), and [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers").

### The Sith Manipulator

At 1st level, this character begins in the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") Heroic Class. They begin with [Linguist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Linguist "Linguist"), [Weapon Proficiency (Pistols)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Pistols) "Weapon Proficiency (Pistols)"), and [Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Simple_Weapons) "Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)") as starting [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), plus the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat. For [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), they begin with **[Wealth](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wealth "Wealth")** from the [Lineage Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lineage_Talent_Tree "Lineage Talent Tree"). They also begin with [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception"), [Gather Information](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gather_Information "Gather Information"), [Knowledge (Bureaucracy)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Bureaucracy) "Knowledge (Bureaucracy)"), [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception"), [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion"), and [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") (plus any additional based on their [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") bonus). The [Corruption](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Corruption "Corruption") Destiny is their path.

By the time they reach 3rd level, they have added the [Strong in the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strong_in_the_Force "Strong in the Force") and [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)") Feats, as well as the **[Presence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Presence "Presence")** Talent from the [Influence Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Influence_Talent_Tree "Influence Talent Tree").

By 5th level, they have increased both their [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") and [Charisma](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charisma "Charisma") attributes by one point each. They also add the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat and the **[Weaken Resolve](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weaken_Resolve "Weaken Resolve")** Talent from the [Influence Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Influence_Talent_Tree "Influence Talent Tree"). The [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat confers one [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power"), *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* (plus, most likely, a few extra based on the character's [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") bonus).

At 7th level, they complete the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") Heroic Class and enter the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class, finally reaching their goal. They either have behaved in such a way to garner the appropriate number of [Dark Side Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Points "Dark Side Points") or will automatically get them by falling to the corruption of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") and completing their [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"). They have added the **[Force Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Deception "Force Deception")** Talent from the [Sith Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Talent_Tree "Sith Talent Tree") and the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat twice more, giving them *[Force Lightning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Lightning "Force Lightning")* and *[Rebuke](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebuke "Rebuke")* (plus possible others based on their [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") bonus). They also choose a new [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"), either [Destruction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destruction "Destruction") or [Discovery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Discovery "Discovery").

### The Sith Warrior

At 1st level, this character begins in the [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") Heroic Class. They begin with [Armor Proficiency (Light)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor_Proficiency_(Light) "Armor Proficiency (Light)"), [Armor Proficiency (Medium)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor_Proficiency_(Medium) "Armor Proficiency (Medium)"), [Weapon Proficiency (Pistols)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Pistols) "Weapon Proficiency (Pistols)"), [Weapon Proficiency (Rifles)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Rifles) "Weapon Proficiency (Rifles)"), and [Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Simple_Weapons) "Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)") as starting [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), plus the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat. For [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), they begin with **[Armored Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armored_Defense "Armored Defense")** from the [Armor Specialist Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor_Specialist_Talent_Tree "Armor Specialist Talent Tree"). They also begin with [Initiative](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Initiative "Initiative"), [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception"), and [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") (plus any additional [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills") based on their [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") bonus). The [Corruption](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Corruption "Corruption") Destiny is their path.

By the time they reach 3rd level, they have added the [Weapon Focus (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Focus_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Focus (Lightsabers)") and [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)") Feats, as well as the **[Penetrating Attack (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Penetrating_Attack_(Lightsabers) "Penetrating Attack (Lightsabers)")** Talent from the [Weapon Specialist Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Specialist_Talent_Tree "Weapon Specialist Talent Tree").

By 5th level, they have increased both their [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") and [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") attributes by one point each. They also add the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat and the **[Power of the Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Power_of_the_Dark_Side "Power of the Dark Side")** Talent from the [Dark Side Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Talent_Tree "Dark Side Talent Tree"). The [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat confers one [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power"), *[Dark Rage](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Rage "Dark Rage")* (plus possible others based on the character's [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") bonus).

At 7th level, they complete the [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") Heroic Class and enters the [Sith Apprentice](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Apprentice "Sith Apprentice") Prestige Class, finally reaching their goal. Like the Sith Manipulator, they either have behaved in such a way to garner the appropriate number of [Dark Side Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Points "Dark Side Points") or will automatically get them by falling to the corruption of [The Sith](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Sith "The Sith") and completing their [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny"). They also add the **[Deflect](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deflect "Deflect")** Talent from the [Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber_Combat_Talent_Tree "Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree"), either the [Dual Weapon Mastery I](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dual_Weapon_Mastery_I "Dual Weapon Mastery I") or [Power Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Power_Attack "Power Attack") Feat (depending on whether their [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") or [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") qualifies), and the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat, which gives them the *[Force Slam](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Slam "Force Slam")* Force Power. They also choose a new [Corruption](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Corruption "Corruption") or [Destruction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destruction "Destruction") Destiny.

### The Fallen Jedi

At 1st level, this character begins in the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") Heroic Class. They begin with [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity"), [Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Lightsabers) "Weapon Proficiency (Lightsabers)"), and [Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Proficiency_(Simple_Weapons) "Weapon Proficiency (Simple Weapons)") as starting [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), plus the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat, which yields them the Force Power *[Surge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Surge "Surge")* (plus possible others based on their [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") bonus). For [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), they begin with the **[Deflect](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deflect "Deflect")** Talent from the [Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber_Combat_Talent_Tree "Lightsaber Combat Talent Tree"). They also begin with [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") and [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") as [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") (plus any additional [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills") based on their [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") bonus). The [Corruption](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Corruption "Corruption") Destiny is their path.

By the time they reach 3rd level, they have added the [Force Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Training "Force Training") Feat twice, which gives them the Force Powers *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* and *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* (plus possible others based on their [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") modifier) and the **[Force Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Perception "Force Perception")** Talent from the [Sense Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sense_Talent_Tree "Sense Talent Tree").

By 5th level, they have increased both their [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") and [Wisdom](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Wisdom "Wisdom") attributes by one point each.
