**Q54:** Does [Bantha Herder](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bantha_Herder "Bantha Herder") work with [Grenade](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grenade "Grenade") attacks? If so, what's the point of the [Forceful Blast](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Forceful_Blast "Forceful Blast") Feat?

**A:** Unless an ability that activates on [Ranged Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ranged_Attacks "Ranged Attacks") further clarifies that it activates on [Area Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Area_Attacks "Area Attacks"), assume that it doesn't. The two Feats' abilities are meant to be mutually exclusive.

**Q55:** Does the [Dive for Cover](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dive_for_Cover "Dive for Cover") Feat make you immune to the incoming attack if you dive behind [Total Cover](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Total_Cover "Total Cover")?

**A:** Yes.

**Q56:** Does the [Unified Squadron](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unified_Squadron "Unified Squadron") Team Feat work at [Starship Scale](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Scale "Starship Scale")?

**A:** Yes.

**Q57:** Does having [Tae-Jitsu Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tae-Jitsu_Training "Tae-Jitsu Training") grant a dodge bonus against three targets (two for Tae-Jitsu and one for [Dodge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dodge "Dodge"))?

**A:** You only get two dodge targets, one for [Tae-Jitsu Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tae-Jitsu_Training "Tae-Jitsu Training") and one for [Dodge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dodge "Dodge").

**Q58:** Are the martial arts basics [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") meant to be prerequisites for the [Martial Arts Master](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Martial_Arts_Master "Martial Arts Master") Prestige Class' expertise [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents")?

**A:** No. It was a deliberate design decision to exclude them as prerequisites. This is just another case of ability names not meaning much in Saga.

**Q59:** Can you add [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots") to a [Cybernetic Prosthesis](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cybernetic_Prosthesis "Cybernetic Prosthesis") via the [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist") Feat or by enlarging the prosthesis and, if so, does this change the appearance of said prosthesis?

**A:** Yes, as a DEVICE, you can add [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots") to them just like you could any other device. Doing so, however, voids the cybernetic replacement's warranty (that's a joke) and makes it highly visible to onlookers.

**Q60:** What is the effect of adding the [Enhanced Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Enhanced_Strength "Enhanced Strength") device option of the [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist") Feat to a cybernetic arm? Does it increase the character's [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength")?

**A:** Doing so does not enhance the character's personal [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") score.

**Q61:** How does [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist") interact with the [Total-Replacement Cyborg](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Total-Replacement_Cyborg "Total-Replacement Cyborg")? Could you increase your [Ability Scores](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Scores "Ability Scores") using the [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") options under [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist")?

**A:** No. You treat the [Total-Replacement Cyborg](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Total-Replacement_Cyborg "Total-Replacement Cyborg") as 6 individual DEVICES. They are not treated as a DROID in part or in whole.

**Q62:** If I get a 20 or a 25 on my [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") check to activate *[Battle Strike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Strike "Battle Strike")* do I deal 3d6 and 4d6 damage, respectively? Or is it 2d6 and 3d6? I ask because the text says "an additional." In addition to what? To the attacks damage? Or to the DC 15 bonus damage?

**A:** That's just showing that the damage scales up. It's 1d6/2d6/3d6 extra on top of your normal weapon damage.

**Q63:** Can I use a [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point") to bring back a [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power") (say *[Negate Energy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Negate_Energy "Negate Energy")*) to my suite as a [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") when attacked, and then use said power as a [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") (say, to negate the incoming blaster attack)?

**A:** Yes you may.

**Q64:** Several Force Skills say they work out to 6 squares or to [Line of Sight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Line_of_Sight "Line of Sight"). What does this mean exactly? Can I *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* someone 4 squares away if I am trapped in a dark chest?

**A:** Some [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers") require [Line of Sight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Line_of_Sight "Line of Sight") **and** a limited range, which means someone in a box can't use them. Other powers require [Line of Sight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Line_of_Sight "Line of Sight") **or** a limited range, which means someone in a box can use them on targets that are sufficiently close. (They still have to be able to observe the target via [Sense Surroundings](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sense_Surroundings "Sense Surroundings"), remote surveillance, or what have you.)

**Q65:** If I use a [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point") to regain a [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power"), what [Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Action "Action") does it use? There was contradictory text in the book I believe.

**A:** It's a [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction").

**Q66:** Can you use the *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* power to "fly" yourself around?

**A:** No. You need **[Flight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Flight "Flight")** for that. You also cannot fly by holding on to an object targeted by the *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* power. The buddy system doesn't work either. A friend may use *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* to lift you off the ground, but they cannot do so if they are being flown via *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* himself by another (such as by you).

**Q67:** Can you use the *[Vital Transfer](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vital_Transfer "Vital Transfer")* power on yourself?

**A:** No.

**Q69:** *[Rebuke](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebuke "Rebuke")* can deflect or reverse "one [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power")." Can it also deflect or reverse a [Force Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Talent "Force Talent") like **[Dark Healing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Healing "Dark Healing")**, or a skill roll like [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force")/[Sense Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sense_Force "Sense Force")?

**A:** No. *[Rebuke](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebuke "Rebuke")* only works against [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers"), not against [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills"), or other abilities that aren't [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers").

**Q71:** If I use *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* to throw a [Stormtrooper](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stormtrooper "Stormtrooper") at another [Stormtrooper](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stormtrooper "Stormtrooper"), is the damage going to be 2d6 no matter what I get on my [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") check? Or can it go as high as 16d6 with a good roll?

**A:** Assuming your check was high enough to hurl the object in the first place, the damage is always based on your roll. Both of your [Stormtroopers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stormtroopers "Stormtroopers") would take 16d6 damage provided you hit your target.

**Q72:** What exactly qualifies an "unpalatable suggestion" as referred to in the *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* Force Power? If a squad of [Stormtroopers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stormtroopers "Stormtroopers") is shooting at you, can you *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* the leader into thinking you are an ally? would their squad question them? Will it work against enemies who are active against you?

**A:** This is one reason why we have a Gamemaster capable of making decisions on the fly. *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* makes an unpalatable suggestion seem reasonable- it does NOT mean that the person will automatically do what you want. A [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") check may still be called for if even the reasonable suggestion seems like something the target may not do. *[Mind Tricking](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Tricking "Mind Tricking")* the [Stormtrooper](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stormtrooper "Stormtrooper") leader into thinking you are an ally may work, depending on the situation. In the middle of a firefight, it might distract them for one round, but then they're going to realize that their buddies are shooting at you and figure it out pretty quick.

**Q73:** What is *[Force Shield](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Shield "Force Shield")* supposed to be in [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") to? What's more, does it really NOT protect against the incoming attack (see the [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") errata) that it (hopefully) is meant to react to?

**A:** *[Force Shield](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Shield "Force Shield")* is meant to be in [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") to an incoming attack, though as written it can be in [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") to anybody else's action (whether or not it is an attack). *[Force Shield](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Shield "Force Shield")* does protect against the incoming attack. The errata isn't wrong, but the *[Force Shield](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Shield "Force Shield")* text could be more clear.

**Q74:** Under "Using Force Points," you can lower your [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") by 1 as a [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action"). However, when this process is described (under "[Atoning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atoning "Atoning")") it is said it "represents a period of meditation, reflection and absolution" that can be worked into the campaign or take place between adventures. This seems to imply a much longer process than just a [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action"). Is the [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action") incorrect, or is this period of meditation a prerequisite to taking the [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action") (similar to spending a [Full-Round Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Full-Round_Action "Full-Round Action") to attune a [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") after crafting it), or is this deliberately left as something for the GM's discretion?

**A:** The [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action") is correct. If you want to rule that you have to meditate, that's fine.

**Q75:** When you use a [Free Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Free_Action "Free Action") to spend a [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point") to add 1d6 to a check can you decide to do so after the check has been made, or do you have to declare it before making the d20 roll?

**A:** You can declare it after you make the roll but before you know the result of the check. (For example you roll a 3. Before your GM tells you that a 3 isn't enough to succeed, you can opt to spend a [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point").)

**Q76:** When can someone spend a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point")? Does it have to be directly related to fulfilling one's [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny")? Does it require GM approval?

**A:** You can spend a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") at any time provided you have one. As to whether or not it must be related to your [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny") is something that should be taken up with your GM and can vary from group to group. Technically, everything requires the approval of your GM. By the rules as written, no it does not need to be related to your [Destiny](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny "Destiny").

**Q77:** Can you have a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") war? In other words, could I use a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") to say that I hit an opponent and then they use a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") to say that the hit failed, but then I use another point to say I hit, and so on and so forth?

**A:** Yes and no. A [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") spent to cause your attack to miss makes the attack miss (even if you spent a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") for an automatic Natural 20). You cannot then spend another [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") to have your missed attack suddenly hit again. However, you could have a "[Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") War" of sorts by spending even more [Destiny Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Points "Destiny Points") to gain an extra round worth of [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions"), which you could then use to make even more attacks (which you could make Natural 20s with more [Destiny Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Points "Destiny Points")). Note that you cannot interrupt somebody else's turn by spending a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") (unless you have the [Quicken Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Quicken_Power "Quicken Power") Force Secret).

**Q78:** How does [Atoning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atoning "Atoning") to rid one's self of [Dark Side Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Points "Dark Side Points") work exactly?

**A:** The player tells you that their character is attempting to [Atone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atone "Atone"), and they spend 1 [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point"). Next, you (the GM) decide how to adjudicate the period of meditation, absolution, reflection, etc.- but this period is definitely not instantaneous. It should span, at minimum, the length of an adventure (during which the PC would have to be a total saint) if you want to play out the absolution "on stage"; alternatively, it would require a substantial period (at least a week, maybe even months) between adventures if you want to leave it off-stage. (You could always do both in combination, letting the [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") take place during multiple adventures and the downtime in between, but I normally wouldn't make it take quite this long.) The length of the atonement period is 100% under the GM's control- it ends when you say it does, and not one second before. (If you have an overriding story-related reason for allowing a very short [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") period, feel free- but unless it's essential to the story, I would strongly recommend following the guidelines I mentioned above.) On top of that, the GM has the final say on whether the [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") is successful- if the hero spends a [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point") to [Atone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atone "Atone") but doesn't improve their behavior, the GM is within their rights to declare that the [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") was unsuccessful and the [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point") lost. (Your players will probably take it more seriously once you point this out to them!)

**One last detail here:** You can only [Atone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atone "Atone") one point at a time. This means that you can't just spend all your leftover [Force Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Points "Force Points") just before you gain a level (unless you get a LOT of downtime between levels, and even then I'd wouldn't be inclined to allow that much [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") without some "on stage" time to prove that the character has sincerely changed their ways). You can probably spend one to start the [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") process before you gain a level (but not in the middle of an encounter- it's a long action, not something that plays out in the normal round structure); still, you wouldn't have time to finish that [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") before you gain a level and get a new set of [Force Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Points "Force Points"), so all the other leftover [Force Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Points "Force Points") will just be lost.

**Bottom line:** The GM, and only the GM, controls the throttle on this sort of thing. If the PCs are "[Atoning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atoning "Atoning")" and then continuing the same behavior, then you're entitled to require them to meet a higher standard (either waiting until they show [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") on-stage before granting the benefits or declaring the [Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement") attempt a failure, wasting the [Force Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Point "Force Point")).

**Q79:** What is the [Dark Side Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Score "Dark Side Score") supposed to represent exactly?

**A:** It represents you being psychologically damaged by your actions (guilt, shame, anger at feeling "forced" to do it, fear over being discovered, etc.). Your need to justify it or bury the pain slowly corrupts you, twisting your reasoning, because the only way to live with it is to do one of two things:

**[Atonement](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atonement "Atonement"):** Take the time to heal, to reflect on what you felt you had to do, and to genuinely make strides to make yourself a better person.

**Live with it:** Just justify your actions to yourself- "I had no choice," "The was no other way," "The ends justify the means"- or bury them in a terrible little hole in your mind to ignore the nagging, terrible reality of what you did. Either way, if the behavior continues, you might find yourself coming up with elaborate justification for almost anything (e.g. killing younglings because they might come back to fight you when they grow up)- or, just as bad, the layers and layers of emotional and psychological scars buried and ignored just drive you beyond rationality altogether, driving you into abyssal depths of madness. In fact, something can be ethically or morally justified but still earn a [Dark Side Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Point "Dark Side Point")! For example, from a moral or ethical point of view, using *[Force Lightning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Lightning "Force Lightning")* to save a busload of orphans from certain death is acceptable, but you will still get a [Dark Side Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Point "Dark Side Point"). ([*[Dark Side](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side "Dark Side")*] Force Powers tap abilities some would call "unnatural"... essentially, you are scarred by being the conduit through which [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") itself is twisted and damaged.) Still, morally and ethically, this would be the only acceptable choice if it was indeed the only way to save all those lives: you're willingly putting your own psychological health at risk for the sake of saving others, so this is clearly an altruistic act. Still, you're damaged by it, so you get the [Dark Side Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Point "Dark Side Point"). The same thing applies in the second example JD gave (Jedi Counseling 44): Even if you really have absolutely no way to save countless lives without resorting to torture, you still get the [Dark Side Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dark_Side_Point "Dark Side Point") because of the damage such a horrible act does to you. You should feel guilty for having to do something like that, and you should carry that pain with you until you have the time to heal and [Atone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atone "Atone"). You might truly believe that you did the right thing and you might have really saved millions or billions of lives, but you still have to face the horror of your own actions.

**Q80:** When purchasing a license for 10 [Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grenades "Grenades"), do I only need to buy it once? Or 10 times? Do I need separate licenses for [Ion Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion_Grenades "Ion Grenades") and [Stun Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun_Grenades "Stun Grenades") (or any other similar weapons for that matter)? Or does one license apply to both?

**A:** It's like having a driver's license; you don't need one for each car you own. If you have a license for a weapon, you have a license for every one of that weapon you have. However, each weapon needs its own license; so, [Heavy Blaster Pistols](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heavy_Blaster_Pistols "Heavy Blaster Pistols") and [Blaster Pistols](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blaster_Pistols "Blaster Pistols") have separate licenses.

**Q81:** [Combat Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Combat_Gloves "Combat Gloves") are considered [Simple Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapons "Simple Weapons"). However they are listed in the [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") section on the table on page 123 and they base their damage on your [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") damage, making them a somewhat unusual weapon. Do feats and talents that apply to simple weapons also apply to [Combat Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Combat_Gloves "Combat Gloves")? What about those that apply to [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed")? Do they stack? For example, what happens if a character wearing [Combat Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Combat_Gloves "Combat Gloves") has both [Weapon Focus (Simple Weapons)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Focus_(Simple_Weapons) "Weapon Focus (Simple Weapons)") and [Weapon Focus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Focus "Weapon Focus") (Unarmed Strike)? What if he had both [Double Attack (Simple Weapons)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Double_Attack_(Simple_Weapons) "Double Attack (Simple Weapons)") and [Triple Crit](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Triple_Crit "Triple Crit") (Unarmed Strike)? By a strict reading of the rules, I would guess that only the [Simple Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapon "Simple Weapon") Feats apply, although I could see applying both [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") but not allowing them to stack.

**A:** [Combat Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Combat_Gloves "Combat Gloves") count as both [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") AND [Simple Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapons "Simple Weapons").

**Q82:** Do [Ion Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion_Grenades "Ion Grenades") destroy the [Equipment](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Equipment "Equipment") of everyone in the area (assuming a good enough attack roll and damage rolls)? What about a [Frag Grenade](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Frag_Grenade "Frag Grenade") or other area of effect attack? Will that damage both the creatures in the area and their [Equipment](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Equipment "Equipment")?

**A:** No on all accounts. [Ion Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion_Grenades "Ion Grenades") and other area effect attacks (such as [Frag Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Frag_Grenades "Frag Grenades") and [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attacks) will damage or destroy unattended objects, but otherwise has no effect on attended objects (that is, anything connected to your body such as in your hand, in your holster, or in your backpack).

**Q84:** Does the extra bonus from **[Attune Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attune_Armor "Attune Armor")**, **[Second Skin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Second_Skin "Second Skin")**, and the [Tech Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tech_Specialist "Tech Specialist") ([Protective Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Protective_Armor "Protective Armor")) get halved by the **[Improved Armored Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Improved_Armored_Defense "Improved Armored Defense")** Talent?

**A:** Yes. The bonuses from those abilities are added to the Armor bonus before the total armor bonus is halved by **[Improved Armored Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Improved_Armored_Defense "Improved Armored Defense")**.

**Q85:** [Nets](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nets "Nets") allow [Grabbing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbing "Grabbing"), [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling"), and [Pinning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinning "Pinning") from range. Can I also maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") while using a [Net](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Net "Net")?

**A:** Yes. [Nets](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nets "Nets") usually have a trailing rope (similar to a lasso) that can be used to maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") from a distance; however this trailing rope is only 6 squares long. Beyond that range, you can still [Throw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Throw "Throw") the [Net](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Net "Net") and [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") an enemy, but you can't maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"). After 1 round, the target would be [Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled") but no longer [Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned"), and would be able to escape with a [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") or [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") check.

**Q86:** Can you [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") multiple targets with an [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attack?

**A:** Not unless the weapon specifically says you can. [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") and [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") are two separate settings and are generally mutually exclusive.

**Q87:** There are no rules text for Gungan [Energy Balls](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Energy_Balls "Energy Balls")! Are they [Thrown Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Thrown_Weapons "Thrown Weapons") or [Simple Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapons "Simple Weapons") for purposes of [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range")? Do you add your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") modifier to damage with them? Can you load them into the [Grenade Launcher](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grenade_Launcher "Grenade Launcher")? Do they only effect [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids")? How long does it take to pull them out to [Throw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Throw "Throw")? How long does it take to load them into the [Atlatl](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atlatl "Atlatl") and [Cesta](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cesta "Cesta")?

**A:** For the purposes of [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") it's treated as a [Thrown Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Thrown_Weapon "Thrown Weapon") if [Thrown](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Thrown "Thrown") by hand, a [Simple Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapon "Simple Weapon") if hurled by an [Atlatl](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Atlatl "Atlatl"), or an [Accurate](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Accurate "Accurate") Simple Weapon if hurled by a [Cesta](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cesta "Cesta"). It functions exactly like all other ranged weapons: if [Thrown](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Thrown "Thrown"), you add you [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") bonus just like you would with other [Thrown Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Thrown_Weapons "Thrown Weapons"), otherwise it's treated just like any other ranged [Simple Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapon "Simple Weapon"). They are not are effect weapons and only effect a single target. They effect all manner of targets and cannot be used in conjunction with [Grenade Launchers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grenade_Launchers "Grenade Launchers") (since they are not [Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grenades "Grenades")).

**Q88:** How long do [Energy Cells](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Energy_Cells "Energy Cells") last? Do things like a [Holorecorder](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Holorecorder "Holorecorder") or [Glow Rod](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Glow_Rod "Glow Rod") need them? Presumably, these have differing operating times with one [Energy Cell](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Energy_Cell "Energy Cell").

**A:** Indefinitely.

**Q89:** Are we really to believe that an unlit [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") is a Medium weapon for the purposes of concealing it with the [Stealth](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stealth "Stealth") Skill? What about other weapons? The size penalties don't seem to make sense in most of their cases either. Should we be using a different scale than that on the weapon's chart to determine the penalties, such as the sizes presented to us in the [Falling Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Falling_Object "Falling Object") chart? I refuse to believe that my [Blaster Pistol](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blaster_Pistol "Blaster Pistol") is the same size as an [Ewok](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ewok "Ewok").

**A:** [Weapon Sizes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Sizes "Weapon Sizes") are there to be relative to the character using it. Concealing an object should rely on the actual size of the object, not its weapon size. So, yeah, a [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") is about Tiny in size when deactivated. I do wish we'd put a bit better size chart in the book, but there's one on the GM screen.

**Q90:** How many [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots") does my [Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor "Armor") have?

**A:** All [Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor "Armor") have 1 [Upgrade Slot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slot "Upgrade Slot") (regardless of size), just like any other item. Powered Armors are an exception and have 2 [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots") instead. There are other exceptions as well as noted in their individual [Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor "Armor") rules text.

**Q91:** How many times can I strip an item to get [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots")?

**A:** Regardless of the manner of stripping you choose, you can only strip an item once. Note that you can also increase an item's size (once) to gain an additional [Upgrade Slot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slot "Upgrade Slot").

**Q92:** Can I add an upgrade to an item being [Dual Geared](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dual_Geared "Dual Geared") into another item via the [Equipment Upgrade](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Equipment_Upgrade "Equipment Upgrade") rules from *[Scum and Villainy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scum_and_Villainy "Scum and Villainy")*?

**A:** No. When you [Dual Gear](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dual_Gear "Dual Gear") an item, the second, smaller item is an upgrade in its own right (rather than an actual item) and thus has no [Upgrade Slots](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Upgrade_Slots "Upgrade Slots") of its own with which to upgrade.

**Q93:** What is the cost/weight to a proton torpedo for the [Miniature Proton Torpedo Launcher](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Miniature_Proton_Torpedo_Launcher "Miniature Proton Torpedo Launcher") shown in the *[Rebellion Era Campaign Guide](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebellion_Era_Campaign_Guide "Rebellion Era Campaign Guide")*? There are no values listed.

**A:** They should weigh about 5 kg and cost 200 credits each.

**Q94:** Are [Gas Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gas_Grenades "Gas Grenades") (*[Rebellion Era Campaign Guide](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebellion_Era_Campaign_Guide "Rebellion Era Campaign Guide")*) really meant to deal 4d6 [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") damage in addition to their [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track") effect? That seems overtly powerful- and it makes [Stun Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun_Grenades "Stun Grenades") nearly pointless.

**A:** It's an error. [Gas Grenades](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gas_Grenades "Gas Grenades") don't deal damage of any kind- only [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track") penalties. This is a classic example of the conflicting rules where the text beats out the table.

**Q95:** Is the [Flame Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Flame_Cannon "Flame Cannon") really supposed to weigh 0.5 kg?

**A:** It's a typo, the actual weight should be about 15 kg.

**Q96:** The [Mortar Launcher](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mortar_Launcher "Mortar Launcher") says you can load it with magazines, or with single shells by hand. Is this just fluff? Or are there game mechanics behind it?

**A:** It's mostly just flavor.

**Q97:** Do you still get the penalty reduction when [Bracing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bracing "Bracing") a [Rotary Blaster Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rotary_Blaster_Cannon "Rotary Blaster Cannon") to benefit from the 2x4 area effect?

**A:** You still get the normal benefits for [Bracing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bracing "Bracing").

**Q98:** Is the [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") supposed to work like the [Blast Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blast_Cannon "Blast Cannon") (such as having a [Splash](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Splash "Splash") area, etc.)?

**A:** No. The [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") is not meant to have a [Splash](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Splash "Splash") and is not meant to be anywhere near as powerful as the [Blast Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blast_Cannon "Blast Cannon") or any other blaster weapon (though it makes a nice, cheap alternative for NPCs or heroes without access to high-end licenses).

**Q99:** Is the [Katarn-Class Commando Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Katarn-Class_Commando_Armor "Katarn-Class Commando Armor") supposed to be [Medium Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Armor "Medium Armor") or [Heavy Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heavy_Armor "Heavy Armor")?

**A:** It is meant to be a [Medium Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Armor "Medium Armor"). If you have the [Armor Proficiency (Heavy)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor_Proficiency_(Heavy) "Armor Proficiency (Heavy)") Feat you gain the benefits of the [Helmet Package](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Helmet_Package "Helmet Package").

**Q100:** The [Ascension Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ascension_Gun "Ascension Gun") reads as though it could be used as an add-on to another weapon. What is the upgrade cost for it?

**A:** There isn't one. The best way to use it is as an "add-on" to an existing weapon would be to use the [Dual Gear](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dual_Gear "Dual Gear") upgrade. There are no plans to include it as a stand alone modification.

**Q101:** What are the mechanical effects of shooting a target (that is, a character or [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid")) with the [Ascension Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ascension_Gun "Ascension Gun")?

**A:** You cannot normally shoot something that is not a solid object with the ascension cable. Shooting a living target or [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") with it, and the resulting effects, is strictly under the purview of the GM.

**Q102:** Can you still fire an [Ascension Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ascension_Gun "Ascension Gun") while traveling along the zip line?

**A:** No.

**Q103:** What is the [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") and blast radius of the [Glowing Defoliators](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Glowing_Defoliators "Glowing Defoliators") in both [Character Scale](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Character_Scale "Character Scale") and [Starship Scale](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Scale "Starship Scale")? What are the weapon's effects on living equipment and ships (such as those used by the [Yuuzhan Vong](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Yuuzhan_Vong "Yuuzhan Vong"))?

**A:** The [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") and radius is unknown at this time as Rodney was away from his notes, though he is aware that this is something that needs to be clarified in the future. It effects Vong gear and ships exactly as it does normal humanoid organisms (that is to say 4d10x2 damage on impact and every round thereafter).

**Q104:** As per its text, a [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") can hit a target at Medium [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range"), but deals no damage. Can I activate [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents"), and other abilities that activate on a hit (but don't need to deal damage to work) when shooting someone with a [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") at Medium [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range")?

**A:** No. Such abilities don't activate when shooting someone with a [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") at Medium [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range"). The reason a [Scatter Gun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scatter_Gun "Scatter Gun") does no damage at Medium [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") is because it isn't hitting anything.

**Q105:** How do you mount a [Shock Stick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shock_Stick "Shock Stick") to a [Rifle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rifle "Rifle") when the [Shock Stick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shock_Stick "Shock Stick") itself is Large?

**A:** You basically take the business end of the [Shock Stick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shock_Stick "Shock Stick") and mount it to your [Rifle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rifle "Rifle") in the same manner as a [Bayonet](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bayonet "Bayonet"). In other words, it is still a large weapon, but your [Rifle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rifle "Rifle") basically makes up the Shock Stick's handle.

**Q106:** How does the [Bothan Weapon Template](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bothan_Weapon_Template "Bothan Weapon Template") (KotoR) interact with [Shockboxing Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shockboxing_Gloves "Shockboxing Gloves")?

**A:** There is no interaction between the two since the [Shockboxing Gloves](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Shockboxing_Gloves "Shockboxing Gloves") don't deal their own damage (rather they just increase your own [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") damage). Rodney and Ryan both unintentionally imply that you don't gain the benefits of the [Bothan Weapon Template](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bothan_Weapon_Template "Bothan Weapon Template") if you have no lethal damage to reduce.

**Q107:** Can you summon a ship with recall [Slave Circuitry](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Slave_Circuitry "Slave Circuitry") with a [Subcutaneous Comlink](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Subcutaneous_Comlink "Subcutaneous Comlink")?

**A:** Anything you can do with a normal [Comlink](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Comlink "Comlink") you can do with a [Subcutaneous Comlink](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Subcutaneous_Comlink "Subcutaneous Comlink") (and do it silently).

**Q108:** Can upgrades be added after installing a prosthesis? Or do you have to upgrade them before they are installed?

**A:** Either way is viable.

**Q109:** When adding [Droid Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid_Systems "Droid Systems") to your [Total-Replacement Cyborg](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Total-Replacement_Cyborg "Total-Replacement Cyborg") body, does the weight of the systems count against your [Carrying Capacity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Carrying_Capacity "Carrying Capacity")?

**A:** Yes they do. The prosthesis themselves don't weigh against you, but additional [Droid Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid_Systems "Droid Systems") do.

**Q110:** Does the damage bonus from your Character Levels add on to all attacks? Or is it selective? Can you apply it to [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attacks? How about [Flamethrower](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Flamethrower "Flamethrower") attacks? An Ithorian's Bellow? A darksider's *[Force Lightning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Lightning "Force Lightning")*?

**A:** Yes to all except *[Force Lightning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Lightning "Force Lightning")* since it is a skill check instead of an attack roll.

**Q111:** Is activating a [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber") a [Free Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Free_Action "Free Action") or a [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action")? The rules seem to contradict themselves in a few places. Also, what kind of [Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Action "Action") is it to flick a switch? (Hint: These questions are related.)

**A:** Swift. Activating an item (including [Lightsabers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsabers "Lightsabers")) is a [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action"). The combat example is erroneous.

**Q112:** Do you get double your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") bonus to your damage when wielding a [Double Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Double_Weapon "Double Weapon")?

**A:** Not if you attempt to attack with both ends in the same round (you would only get x1 your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") bonus on either attack). If you only make one attack for the round, however, you can add double your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") bonus.

**Q114:** Can you [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge") and [Tumble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tumble "Tumble") as part of the [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge") Action?

**A:** No, as they are two separate [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions")...

*"**Tumble (Trained Only):** If you succeed at a DC 15 [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") check, you can Tumble through the [Threatened Area](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threatened_Area "Threatened Area") or [Fighting Space](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fighting_Space "Fighting Space") of an enemy as part of your [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action") without provoking an [Attack of Opportunity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_of_Opportunity "Attack of Opportunity").*

*"**Charge:** As a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"), you can move your [Speed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speed "Speed") (minimum 2 squares) in a straight line through unobstructed terrain, and then make a [Melee Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Attack "Melee Attack") at the end of your movement."*

Since you are using a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") to [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge"), you can not use [Tumble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tumble "Tumble") because it requires a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action"). However, since [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge") is a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"), you still do have a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action") left. So you can use your [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action") and [Tumble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tumble "Tumble") before or after the [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge"), just not as part of the [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge").

**Q115:** If I target an opposing character with *[Move Object](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Object "Move Object")* or [Bantha Rush](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bantha_Rush "Bantha Rush") and move them out of a square [Threatened](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threatened "Threatened") by one or more of my allies, can they make [Attacks of Opportunity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attacks_of_Opportunity "Attacks of Opportunity") against them?

*A:'* No. A target must willingly move from a [Threatened](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threatened "Threatened") square under their own power to provoke an [Attack of Opportunity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_of_Opportunity "Attack of Opportunity").

**Q116:** Do you apply your [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track") penalties to your [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") since they lower your [Fortitude Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude_Defense "Fortitude Defense")?

**A:** Yes. Any modifiers applied to your [Fortitude Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude_Defense "Fortitude Defense") should also effect your [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") (even temporary ones), be they from [Armor](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Armor "Armor"), the [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track"), [Ability Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Score "Ability Score") increases, etc.

**Q117:** What [Weapon Group](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Group "Weapon Group") includes [Unarmed Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed_Attacks "Unarmed Attacks")? Are you automatically proficient with [Unarmed Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed_Attacks "Unarmed Attacks")? Can you use them with combat [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats") like [Double Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Double_Attack "Double Attack")? Can you use them with [Talent Trees](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent_Trees "Talent Trees") like [Weapon Specialist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Specialist_Talent_Tree "Weapon Specialist Talent Tree") and [Weapon Master](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Master_Talent_Tree "Weapon Master Talent Tree")?

**A:** [Unarmed Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed_Attacks "Unarmed Attacks") are considered [Light](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light "Light") melee [Simple Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Simple_Weapons "Simple Weapons") and can be finessed, used in a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"), or dual wielded.

**Q118:** Can you hold [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") with two hands to double your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") modifier to damage? I would guess not, since you're not actually holding a weapon in both hands, and [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") is probably considered a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon") anyway.

**A:** No you do not get double your [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") modifier with [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") strikes unless a [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat"), [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"), class ability, or racial ability says otherwise.

**Q119:** Do shields help to protect against damaging [Force Powers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Powers "Force Powers") such as *[Force Lightning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Lightning "Force Lightning")* or *[Force Slam](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Slam "Force Slam")*?

**A:** Yes they do.

**Q:** Does [Damage Reduction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction "Damage Reduction") protect against [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") weapons?

**A:** Yes

**Q120:** How does [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") damage work when used against a living creature without cybernetics? What about [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") damage?

**A:** If a normal creature is hit by an attack that does [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") damage, it takes only a quarter of the damage (half because it is an [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") attack and half again because of the inappropriate target). The target does not move down the [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track") regardless of whether or not the [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") is exceeded by the damage. An [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") attack, like a [Stunning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stunning "Stunning") attack, is incapable of killing a living creature.

If a cyborg (a living creature with cybernetic enhancements/replacements), a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid"), or an electronic device is hit by an attack that does [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") damage, it takes only half of the damage to its [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points"). The full unmodified damage is compared to the target's [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") to see if it falls 2 steps down the [Condition Track](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Condition_Track "Condition Track") (1 for overcoming the target's [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold"), and 1 more for being an [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") attack). An [Ion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion "Ion") attack, like a [Stunning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stunning "Stunning") attack, is incapable of killing or destroying its target.

Cyborgs suffer the full effect from a [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") device whereas [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") and other electronic objects are completely immune.

**Q121:** Can you ready a [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action")?

**A:** Technically, you can't. However, you can ALWAYS trade down to a lesser action and [Readying](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readying "Readying") is no exception. Therefore, you could ready a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action") or [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") and then exchange it for the [Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action") you desire. It is also worth noting that [Readying an Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readying_an_Action "Readying an Action") takes a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") in its own right.

**Q122:** If someone moves up to me and makes an attack, and I had a prepared action to move 1 square to the right (still in their [Threatened](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Threatened "Threatened") space), does that cancel their attack?

**A:** No. If you [Ready an Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ready_an_Action "Ready an Action") that will render an opponent's action moot, the opponent does not get to declare a different action. Instead, the action is wasted. (If the [Readied Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readied_Action "Readied Action") doesn't interfere with it, of course, the other action goes off as planned.)

For example, A is several squares from B, and B is holding a big honkin' [Vibro-Axe](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vibro-Axe "Vibro-Axe"). A has initiative and [Readies an Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readies_an_Action "Readies an Action") to [Withdraw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Withdraw "Withdraw") as soon as B makes an attack. B charges- at the end of their movement (i.e. once movement is finished), they declare their attack. A's [Readied Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readied_Action "Readied Action") is triggered, so A [Withdraws](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Withdraws "Withdraws") away from B. B's attack is now rendered moot (A is no longer within their [Reach](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reach "Reach")), so B's attack is essentially canceled. In your example, you are still within [Reach](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reach "Reach") and are still capable of being attacked despite your [Readied Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Readied_Action "Readied Action") to sidestep at the last moment.

**Q124:** When [Aiding Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aiding_Another "Aiding Another") for [Ranged Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ranged_Attacks "Ranged Attacks"), do [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") penalties affect the attempt? What happens with [Weapon Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Batteries "Weapon Batteries") (such as those found on [Capital Ships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ships "Capital Ships"))?

**A:** Yes, [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") penalties apply for [Aid Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aid_Another "Aid Another") checks. The primary [Gunner](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gunner "Gunner") in the assisting [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") must make an attack against [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense") 10 (without the benefit of any [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") fire bonus from their own [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery")), applying [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") modifiers to the attack roll as normal; if successful, the assisted (attacking) [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") gains +2 on its attack roll per gun in the assisting [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery").

**Q125:** When you roll a [Critical Hit](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Critical_Hit "Critical Hit"), do you roll the damage twice (a la D&D) or simply roll once and multiply by 2?

**A:** You simply multiply.

**Q126:** [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling") seems to involve a lot of different rules: [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab"), [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"), [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip"), [Crush](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Crush "Crush"), [Throw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Throw "Throw"), opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") checks, and three different rules on escaping. How does it all work?

**A:** It's actually simpler than it looks! There are only three states: **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**, **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**, and **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**. All the other rules ([Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip"), [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") attack, [Crush](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Crush "Crush"), [Throw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Throw "Throw"), opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check, and escape) are just different ways of moving between the three states.

Untrained attackers can only [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab"). Trained attackers (those who have either the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") or [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") Feat) can improve their [Grabs](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabs "Grabs") into an improved hold called a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"). [Grapples](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapples "Grapples") are harder to escape from, and also allow you to immobilize your enemy, knock them [Prone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prone "Prone"), and even cause damage.

**Q127:** How do you [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") an enemy?

**A:** Make an [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") melee attack roll, as a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"). If successful, the enemy is **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**.

An untrained attacker (one without the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") or [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") Feats) suffers a -5 penalty on the [Melee Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Attack "Melee Attack") roll. A trained attacker (one who has either the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") or [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") Feat) can immediately try to improve their [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") into a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"), as part of the same [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action").

**Q128:** What are the effects of being **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**?

**A:** A **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** enemy cannot move from their square, and also suffers a -2 penalty on attack rolls unless they're using a Natural Weapon or a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon").

The target remains **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** until they escape, or until the attacker improves the [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") into a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple").

**Q129:** How do you [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") an enemy?

**A:** When a trained attacker (one who has the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") or [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") Feats) makes a successful [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") attack, they can immediately try to improve their [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") into a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") as part of the same [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action").

Once a trained attacker has **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** the enemy, both fighters immediately make an opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check. If the attacker's check result equals or exceeds the defender's result, the defender becomes **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**.

**Q130:** What are the effects of being **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**?

**A:** The effects are exactly the same as the **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** state: the target cannot move from their square, and also suffers a -2 penalty on attack rolls unless they're using a Natural Weapon or a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon"). However escape from **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** is more difficult, and the attacker additionally gets the benefit of their chosen [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling") style: [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") (possibly including [Crush](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Crush "Crush")), [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") (possibly including [Throw](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Throw "Throw")), or a close-range hit with a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon").

The target remains **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** until they escape, or until the attacker knocks them [Prone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prone "Prone") with the [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") Feat.

**Q131:** How do you [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") an enemy?

**A:** If the attacker is using the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") Feat, and wins the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check, the target becomes **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**. (Note that any **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** target will always be **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** as well, since the attacker made a successful opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check to perform the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin").)

**Q132:** What are the effects of being **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**?

**A:** A **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** target cannot [Move](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move "Move") or take any [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions"), and also loses their [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") bonus to [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense"). The target remains **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** for one round, until the attacker's next turn.

In each subsequent round, the attacker can try to maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") by making another opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check. If the attacker doesn't maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), the target ceases being **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** but is still **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**.

**Q133:** When trying to maintain a [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), does the attacker have to roll both the [Melee Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Attack "Melee Attack") roll and the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check, each round?

**A:** No. A [Melee Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Attack "Melee Attack") roll is only used to initiate a [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab"). If the target is already **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**, **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**, or **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**, the attacker can proceed directly to the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check.

**Q135:** When the attacker makes an opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check to strike with a [Light](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light "Light") or Natural Weapon, do they have to make an attack roll with the weapon, too?

**A:** No. There are only three die rolls:

- A [Melee Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Attack "Melee Attack") roll to grab (if the target wasn't already **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**, **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**, or **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**)
- An opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check
- Damage dice for the weapon

**Q136:** If the defender wins the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check, does that mean they escape from the [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") or [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple")?

**A:** No. When the defender wins the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check, that only means that the attack has failed: the attacker doesn't get to [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip"), or hit with a Natural or [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon"). However the defender is still either **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** or **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**, exactly as before.

**Q137:** So how does the defender escape from a [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab")? From a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple")? From a [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin")?

**A:** Escaping from **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** is easy: as a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"), the defender can break free from a number of [Grabs](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabs "Grabs") equal to their level. No roll is required; success is automatic.

Escaping from **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** is harder: as a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") during their turn, the defender must make an [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") check (DC = the attacker's last opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check result).

You can escape from **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** only by winning the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check during the attacker's turn. If the attacker fails to maintain their [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"), the hold is reduced to a [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple").

The attacker can also perform [Grabs](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabs "Grabs") and [Grapples](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapples "Grapples") from a distance by using a [Net](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Net "Net"). If **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** or **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** in a [Net](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Net "Net"), escape is a [Full-Round Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Full-Round_Action "Full-Round Action") requiring a DC 15 [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") check or a DC 20 [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") check.

**Q138:** While **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")** or **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** (but not **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**), can I still fight?

**A:** Yes. However you cannot move from your square, and you suffer a -2 penalty on attack rolls unless using a Natural Weapon or a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon").

**Q139:** While **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**, can I still... Fight with a [Light Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Weapon "Light Weapon")? Use a [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power")? Use [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") to escape? Make a [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") check to break free? Use **[Adept Negotiator](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Adept_Negotiator "Adept Negotiator")**? Enter a [Serenity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Serenity "Serenity") trance? **[Block](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Block "Block")** and **[Deflect](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deflect "Deflect")**? Cut my way out with a [Lightsaber](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Lightsaber "Lightsaber")?

**A:** No. While **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**, the defender "can't move or take any [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions")." The only way to escape from a [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") is by winning the opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check on the attacker's turn. Until then, you cannot do anything at all.

**Q140:** Could you please give an example of [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling") and [Pinning](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinning "Pinning")?

**A:** Sure. Say that Amy has the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") Feat, while Bob is the poor schmuck who's getting his arm twisted.

- Round 1 - Amy wants to [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") Bob:
  - She makes an [Unarmed Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed_Attack "Unarmed Attack") roll and succeeds (Bob is now **[Grabbed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grabbed "Grabbed")**).
  - She makes an opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check and succeeds.
  - Bob is now **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**, and also **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** for one round.
  - Bob gets no [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions") for this round because he's **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**.
- Round 2 - Amy wants to maintain the [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin"):
  - She tries an opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check to maintain her [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") for another round, but fails.
  - Bob is no longer **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")**, but he is still **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**.
  - Since Bob isn't **[Pinned](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pinned "Pinned")** anymore, he can act. He tries to escape the [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"):
  - He makes an [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") check and succeeds.
  - Amy is no longer [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling") Bob; he has escaped.
- Round 3 - Amy wants to [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") again, but instead of attempting a [Pin](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pin "Pin") this time, she just wants to stab Bob with a [Knife](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knife "Knife").
  - She makes an [Unarmed Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed_Attack "Unarmed Attack") roll and succeeds.
  - She make an opposed [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") check and succeeds.
  - Bob is **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")** again, and also takes damage from the dagger.
- On Bob's turn, he can attack Amy (at a -2 penalty, since he's **[Grappled](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappled "Grappled")**) or he can attempt escape again with another [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") roll.

**Q141:** Does [Damage Reduction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction "Damage Reduction") stack? For example, would a [Nautolan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nautolan "Nautolan") (with a racial DR 2 due to rubbery hide) have DR 12 when they activate their **[Damage Reduction 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction_10 "Damage Reduction 10")** Talent?

**A:** No. DR of the same kind does not stack. Since a [Nautolan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nautolan "Nautolan") has DR 2/- and the **[Damage Reduction 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction_10 "Damage Reduction 10")** Talent grants DR 10/-, the effects would overlap for a total of DR 10/-.

In the case of two different types of [Damage Reduction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction "Damage Reduction"), you use whichever one is the most appropriate to the situation. For example, if you have DR 2/-, DR 5/-, and DR 10/Energy and were shot by a slugthrower, you would only deduct 10 points of damage from the attack. If it were a [Blaster Pistol](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blaster_Pistol "Blaster Pistol"), however, you would deduct 5 damage from the attack.

**Q142:** Can you combine the [Fighting Defensively](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fighting_Defensively "Fighting Defensively") Action and the [Melee Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Defense "Melee Defense") Feat?

**A:** Yes, you can, providing you are making an attack as a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"). You cannot use [Melee Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Melee_Defense "Melee Defense") when taking the [Full Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Full_Attack "Full Attack") Action. The Feat specifically states that it can be used whenever you use a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") to make an attack.

**Q143:** Can you dual wield [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") strikes?

**A:** Yep! You absolutely can dual wield your [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") strikes as evidenced by the [Trandoshan Mercenary](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trandoshan_Mercenary "Trandoshan Mercenary").

**Q144:** Can you have multiple [Reactions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reactions "Reactions") against the same [Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Action "Action")? For example, could you attempt to **[Deflect](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deflect "Deflect")** a blaster bolt, and then use *[Negate Energy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Negate_Energy "Negate Energy")* if you fail?

**A:** No. That's the big difference between [Reactions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reactions "Reactions") and [Free Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Free_Actions "Free Actions")- you can do as many [Free Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Free_Actions "Free Actions") as the GM will allow, but you only make one [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") to any particular [Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Action "Action"). You may, however, react to multiple [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions") over the course of a round.

**Q145:** Currently there are almost no rules for mounted combat whatsoever. We only have the little bit under the [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") Skill to go off of. How do you fight with a mount exactly?

**A:** The [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") Skill functions much like the [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") Skill insofar as [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") controls a mount and [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") controls a [Speeder Bike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speeder_Bike "Speeder Bike"). The difference is that a mount is a creature all its own; you just manage to control that creature through the use of the [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") Skill. I'll answer the below items, but also keep in mind that mounted combat is certainly one area we can expand upon. The mounted combat rules in the *[Core Rulebook](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Core_Rulebook "Core Rulebook")* are far from the be-all, end-all of what we could do with them.

**Q146:** Does your mount's movement count against you for the turn? Can I, for example, have the mount take the [Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Run "Run") Action (a [Full-Round Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Full-Round_Action "Full-Round Action") for the mount) while I reload my gun (a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action")), switch it to [Stun](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stun "Stun") ([Swift Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Action "Swift Action")), and then shoot at the target pursuing me (a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"))?

**A:** Your mount's movement is its own movement. If you want to control the mount in battle, it takes a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action"). However, mounts specifically trained for battle ignore the rolling requirement, NOT the [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action") requirement. Keep in mind, though, that the above actions would probably not be possible without guiding the mount with your knees to have both hands free, which is another [Skill Check](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Check "Skill Check").

**Q147:** What happens if my mount [Charges](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charges "Charges")? Do I suffer the penalties? Do I get the bonuses?

**A:** As written, you do not suffer the penalties or gain the benefits of a mount's [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge"). This is one of those areas that could certainly be expanded out with [Feats](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feats "Feats"), though, so I could easily see a [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat") that lets you do that.

**Q148:** Can I attack in the same turn that my mount attacks? Do I need to make a [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") check to do so?
