**A:** Sure, you can both attack on the same turn. And you need to make a check only if the mount is not trained for combat.

**Q149:** How do you handle the [Grappling](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grappling "Grappling")/[Tripping](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tripping "Tripping") rules while mounted?

**A:** The same way they work when you ride a [Speeder Bike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speeder_Bike "Speeder Bike"). Typically, a mount provides you with no [Cover](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cover "Cover") against attacks (including [Grab](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grab "Grab") and [Trip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trip "Trip") attacks) unless you use the [Use the Mount as Cover](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Mount_as_Cover "Use the Mount as Cover") application of the [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") Skill.

**Q150:** Also, is [Using the Mount as Cover](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Using_the_Mount_as_Cover "Using the Mount as Cover") a [Reaction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reaction "Reaction") made against an attack? The text doesn't specifically say so.

**A:** Yes

**Q151:** If I make an [Area Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Area_Attack "Area Attack") on a [Droideka Destroyer Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droideka_Destroyer_Droid "Droideka Destroyer Droid"), or a [Starship Scale](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Scale "Starship Scale") Area Attack on an enemy ship, what happens if I miss?

**A:** Just like any attack roll, [Area Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Area_Attacks "Area Attacks") resolve the attack first, damage resolution second. I miss with the attack, so it is halved, and then the damage is applied to shields, DR, and [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points").

**Q152:** Also, if I roll a Natural 1 on my area effect attack roll, do I deal half damage (if I still beat [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense") 10), or do I deal no damage since I "automatically missed?"

**A:** A Natural 1 on an attack roll is always an automatic miss. In this case, you fail to hit the area you attacked.

**Q153:** What does a [Cone](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cone "Cone") in Saga look like? What about a [Burst](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Burst "Burst") or [Splash](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Splash "Splash")?

**A:** For the sake of consistency, area effects have the same shape and area that they did in the revised rules and in Dungeons & Dragons.

[![](//static.wikitide.net/swsewiki/thumb/f/f3/Burst.webp/250px-Burst.webp.png)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/File:Burst.webp)

The Red Squares are a 1-Square Burst. The Blue Squares are a 2-Square Burst. The Green Squares are a 3-Square Burst. The Yellow Squares are a 4-Square Burst.

[![](//static.wikitide.net/swsewiki/thumb/7/74/Cone.webp/250px-Cone.webp.png)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/File:Cone.webp)

The Cone starts at an intersection on the battle grid (one of the corners of your space) and then extends outward for 6 squares in a 90-degree arc. Unlike normal range calculations, however, count diagonals using the old method- that is, 1 square for the first and all odd diagonals, and 2 squares for the second and all even diagonals.

[![](//static.wikitide.net/swsewiki/thumb/2/22/Splash.webp/250px-Splash.webp.png)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/File:Splash.webp)

The red square is no Splash. Blue Squares are 1-Square Splash. X Splash means you have a circle with Radius X. That means the Green Squares are Radius 2, the Yellow Squares are Radius 3, and the Purple Squares are Radius 4.

**Q154:** How would a PC or unique crew affect a Vehicle's stats? Which bonuses would it give?

**A:** You determine the following stats in the following way (in order of stat block appearance):

- **Vehicle [Initiative](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Initiative "Initiative"):** The pilot's [Initiative](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Initiative "Initiative") modifier (or the [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") skill modifier if you are [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained")) + Vehicle's size modifier + Vehicle's [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") modifier.
- **[Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception"):** Character's normal [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception") modifier.
- **[Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense"):** The Vehicle's [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense") is equal to 10 + size modifier + Armor bonus

(or pilot's Heroic Level) + [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") bonus.

- **Attack Rolls:** Gunner's [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus") + Vehicle's [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") modifier + miscellaneous bonuses ([Point-Blank Shot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Point-Blank_Shot "Point-Blank Shot"), [Weapon Focus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Focus "Weapon Focus"), etc.) + 2 (if [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") in [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") and firing [Vehicle Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Pilot "Vehicle Pilot") controlled weapons from the pilot's position).
- **[Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus"):** The gunner's [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus").
- **[Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple"):** A Vehicle's [Grapple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Grapple "Grapple") modifier is equal to its pilot's [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus") + the Vehicle's [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength") modifier + the Vehicle's special size modifier (Large +5, Huge +10, Gargantuan +15, Colossal +20, Colossal (Frigate) +25, Colossal (Cruiser) +30, Colossal (Station) +35).
- **[Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills"):** Character's appropriate [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill") modifiers (+ [Vehicle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle "Vehicle") size modifier + Vehicle [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") modifier in the case of the [Initiative](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Initiative "Initiative") and [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") Skill). If the ship's computer is Friendly or Helpful towards you, you also add the ship's [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") modifier as an [Equipment](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Equipment "Equipment") bonus to your [Use Computer](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_Computer "Use Computer") checks.
- If it is not listed above, than it is not changed by having unique crew or is otherwise explicitly spelled out in the *[Core Rulebook](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Core_Rulebook "Core Rulebook")*.

**Q155:** Can you specialize with spacecraft weapons? Would it count double, and if so, could you use [Point-Blank Shot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Point-Blank_Shot "Point-Blank Shot") for extra damage? I'm assuming **[Penetrating Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Penetrating_Attack "Penetrating Attack")** would still allow you to reduce the DR. Does it work on SR as well?

**A:** Yes you can specialize. Yes you can use [Point-Blank Shot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Point-Blank_Shot "Point-Blank Shot"). Yes **[Penetrating Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Penetrating_Attack "Penetrating Attack")** still gets through [Damage Reduction](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Reduction "Damage Reduction"). No, **[Penetrating Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Penetrating_Attack "Penetrating Attack")** does not get through shields. Yes all damage multiplies (if there is a damage multiplier). Remember though that [Starship Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Weapons "Starship Weapons") are considered [Heavy Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heavy_Weapons "Heavy Weapons") for proficiency, specialization, and so on.

**Q156:** Under "[Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight")" one of the consequences is that you must select the [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") Action each turn. However, there is no [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") Action specifically listed. What exactly can you do in a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") and in what order?

**A:** The [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") Action is listed. It's a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action"), and you have to follow the attacking rules while engaged in a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight").

**Q157:** Most large scale [Vehicle Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapons "Vehicle Weapons") have a multiplier attached. How does this multiplier interact your 1/2 level damage? Would a 10th level character deal (#d6x2)+5 damage? Or (#d6+5)x2 damage?

**A:** Bonus damage is applied before multipliers. Same goes for increasing the number of dice rolled.

**Q159:** When establishing a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") you make an opposed roll, if you succeed you are now [Dogfighting](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfighting "Dogfighting"). Do you:

- a) Then make an attack as a [Free Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Free_Action "Free Action").
- b) Make another opposed check without a penalty to see if you can attack.
- c) Do nothing as you've already taken an [Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Action "Action") and therefore give the foe first attempt at an attack.

**A:** C. You can try to attack on a later action, but the benefit of this initial "begin a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight")" [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") is that the other fighter can't just fly by uninhibited. (Yes, this does mean the other pilot will have the first chance to attack, assuming they want to- they may just try to disengage because they have other things to do.)

**Q161:** If two [TIE Fighters](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/TIE_Fighters "TIE Fighters") are [Dogfighting](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfighting "Dogfighting") an [X-Wing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/X-Wing "X-Wing") can one use [Aid Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aid_Another "Aid Another") for attacks, [Skill Checks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Checks "Skill Checks") and the like?

**A:** Yes, but the [Aid Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aid_Another "Aid Another") attempt can only help the [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") check or the following attack roll, not both- and you have to decide which you're assisting before the aided pilot makes their roll(s).

**Q162:** Since disengaging a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") is a [Move Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Move_Action "Move Action"), are you still required to take the [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") Standard Action as well?

**A:** No. You only have to spend a [Standard Action](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Standard_Action "Standard Action") when initiating a [Dogfight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dogfight "Dogfight") or trying to attack a target.

**Q163:** The [AT-AT](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/AT-AT "AT-AT") is listed as size 6x12. Is it supposed to be 12x12?

**A:** It's meant to match the [AT-AT](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/AT-AT "AT-AT") mini, which is 6x12. You could treat it as 12x2, though, and that would actually be a bit easier in many situations.

**Q164:** Can the [Vehicle Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Pilot "Vehicle Pilot") of a [Starfighter](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starfighter "Starfighter") use the [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") Action?

**A:** Only in one of the following two cases:

- Come to complete stop, then [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") and attack in the same round.
- [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") at the end of round 1, then attack at the beginning of round two. (You can't take any other [Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Actions "Actions") between the two- including [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") checks, [Attacks of Opportunity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attacks_of_Opportunity "Attacks of Opportunity"), or whatever- without losing the benefits of [Aiming](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aiming "Aiming"), though.)

**Q165:** On capital class ships how many crewmen can use the [Aid Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aid_Another "Aid Another") to assist the various ship positions?

**A:** Well, only the [Copilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Copilot "Copilot") can assist [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") checks, and only the [Commander](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Commander "Commander") can assist [Use Computer](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_Computer "Use Computer") checks, so for those two [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills") a maximum of one person can assist. For [Mechanics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mechanics "Mechanics") checks, there's technically no limit, but this is up to the GM. (They might decide to limit the size of damage-control parties, but there's no specific rule for it at this time.)

**Q166:** Can [Vehicles](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicles "Vehicles") use [Suppressing Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Suppressing_Fire "Suppressing Fire")? Can [Vehicles](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicles "Vehicles") be suppressed?

**A:** Yes and yes.

**Q167:** [Recharging Shields](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Recharging_Shields "Recharging Shields") for 5 points makes sense for [Starfighters](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starfighters "Starfighters"), but seems really low for [Capital Ships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ships "Capital Ships"), in RCR larger ships regained more shield points per Recharge than smaller ones. Is the new setup intentional or was something left out?

**A:** This was intentional.

**Q168:** If I use *[Battle Strike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Strike "Battle Strike")* with [Vehicle Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapons "Vehicle Weapons"), do the extra dice get multiplied?

**A:** Yes. Everything multiplies. Note however that when using a [Vehicle Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapon "Vehicle Weapon"), you cannot apply any effect that causes an automatic Natural 20 or automatic [Critical Hit](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Critical_Hit "Critical Hit") (such as spending a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") or using [Serenity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Serenity "Serenity")), unless a rule specifies that it can be used with [Vehicle Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapons "Vehicle Weapons").

**Q169:** Since the [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run") Action involves directing the Vehicle's movement, does it suffice as an act of controlling the [Vehicle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle "Vehicle")? If so, could a [Vehicle Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Pilot "Vehicle Pilot") use two [Swift Actions](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swift_Actions "Swift Actions") to lock on with a torpedo, then make an [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run") to fire it in the same round, without losing control of the [Vehicle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle "Vehicle")? Alternately, could a person with [Charging Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charging_Fire "Charging Fire") Aim and then [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge") and get the benefits of both?

**A:** While an [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run") does count as movement for the purposes of controlling your [Vehicle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle "Vehicle"), you cannot [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") then perform an [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run"). From the description of [Aiming](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aiming "Aiming"), page 154: *You lose the benefits of Aiming if you lose [Line of Sight](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Line_of_Sight "Line of Sight") to your target or **if you take any other action before making your attack.***

Since the [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run") Action is causing you to take another action before the attack, no, you cannot [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") and then gain the benefits on an [Attack Run](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Run "Attack Run"). This is the same reason you cannot [Aim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aim "Aim") and use [Charging Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charging_Fire "Charging Fire") with a [Charge](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Charge "Charge").

**Q170:** In the new *[Starships of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships_of_the_Galaxy "Starships of the Galaxy")* book, some of the existing ships are terribly vague when it comes to telling me what kind of weapons there are. This is important as I need to know precisely what kind they are so I can determine how many [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") I get back when I take them off to add other modifications.

**A:** While I can't help you with things like internal systems (it's just not feasible to list every single system available in a [Capital Ship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ship "Capital Ship"), for example, and a certain amount of GM fiat has to be assumed), I can help with things like weapons. Any time a [Weapon System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_System "Weapon System") is referred to without a size, assume it is a medium version of that system. Thus, the [*Dynamic*-Class Freighter](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dynamic-Class_Freighter "Dynamic-Class Freighter") has one Double [Medium Blaster Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Blaster_Cannon "Medium Blaster Cannon"), worth 1 [Emplacement Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Point "Emplacement Point").

When it comes to removing [Weapon Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Systems "Weapon Systems"), you count it as the number weapons listed in the stat block. If something says "[Laser Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Laser_Cannons "Laser Cannons")" then they are all part of the same individual system; regardless of using a plural word, it is always considered to be a single system. So, for example, since the Medium Transport's stat block clearly mentions 4 Point-Defense Double [Laser Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Laser_Cannons "Laser Cannons"), that means you calculate the [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") for a Point-Defense Double [Medium Laser Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Laser_Cannon "Medium Laser Cannon"), and you have 4 such systems to remove. The [*Citadel*-Class Cruiser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Citadel-Class_Cruiser "Citadel-Class Cruiser") lists a single set of [Laser Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Laser_Cannons "Laser Cannons") (treated as [Medium Laser Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Laser_Cannons "Medium Laser Cannons")), while the ship also has 2 sets of [Ion Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ion_Cannons "Ion Cannons") (treated as [Medium Ion Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Medium_Ion_Cannons "Medium Ion Cannons")). As for [Fire-Linking](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linking "Fire-Linking"), any weapons that have an [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attack that are not listed as [Double](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Double "Double"), [Triple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Triple "Triple"), or [Quad](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Quad "Quad") weapons should be considered to be [Fire-Linked](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linked "Fire-Linked"). If the damage is higher than average for 2 [Fire-Linked](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linked "Fire-Linked") weapons, assume it's 4 [Fire-Linked](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linked "Fire-Linked").

If the damage die codes don't match up exactly to those in the modification chapter, that's a result of decades of RPGs and other technical manuals establishing systems without codifying them. As we say in the book, there's no way to realistically simulate the thousands of factors that go into a Starship's creation in a meaningful system that can be used easily, not and maintain continuity, so that's the sacrifice you have to make. Some die codes for established ships won't match up, but once again all that really matters is that the system be used to balance player modifications.

**Q171:** So I should just assume the [Z-95 Headhunter](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Z-95_Headhunter "Z-95 Headhunter") is using the Double [Blaster Cannons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Blaster_Cannons "Blaster Cannons") option (which is described as a [Triple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Triple "Triple"))? If so, can I take them off to get a few [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") and then spend the same amount of [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") for a new set of triple blasters that will do 4d10x2 instead of 3d10x2?

**A:** Yes.

**Q172:** Can a [Capital Ship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ship "Capital Ship") using the [Focused Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Focused_Fire "Focused Fire") option choose to use an entire [Weapon Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Battery "Weapon Battery") or is it limited to a single weapon (such as a single [Turbolaser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolaser "Turbolaser") from a [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery"))? If they can, do they deal extra dice of damage on a successful hit (as is normal for [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries"))? Obviously, they won't deal extra dice if they miss (in which case they deal only half the base damage).

**A:** They're picking a single [Weapon System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_System "Weapon System"); in the case of [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries"), yes, this includes all the normal aspects of battery fire. Hence, the "takes normal damage" clause, not "takes weapon damage."

**Q173:** How does [Fire-Linking](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linking "Fire-Linking") work? Do I need two guns of the same type (so to [Fire-Link](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Link "Fire-Link"), I need to buy 2 weapons, then the [Fire-Linking](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linking "Fire-Linking") option, making my two [Turbolasers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolasers "Turbolasers") into a single [Weapon System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_System "Weapon System")?) or do I only need one [Turbolaser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolaser "Turbolaser") to start, keeping me from wasting a whole bunch of [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") and money?

**A:** You only need one base weapon and the [Fire-Link](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Link "Fire-Link") trait. For example, if you purchase a single [Light Turbolaser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Turbolaser "Light Turbolaser"), then get [Fire-Link](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Link "Fire-Link") (4), you now have a 5d10x5 quartet of linked [Light Turbolasers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Light_Turbolasers "Light Turbolasers") (which also get the bonus of [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire")).

**Q174:** Can I stack the [Quad](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Quad "Quad"), [Fire-Linked](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linked "Fire-Linked"), and [Cannon Enhancements](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cannon_Enhancements "Cannon Enhancements") abilities on a [Heavy Blaster Cannon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heavy_Blaster_Cannon "Heavy Blaster Cannon") (albeit at ridiculous cost)?

**A:** Yes you can. Many weapons can get as high as 11d10 damage this way. When multiplying the costs, however, you follow the order of operations, making the final cost quite extreme.

**Q175:** How do you go about adding [Weapon Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Batteries "Weapon Batteries") to a ship? The tables have [Autoblasters](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autoblasters "Autoblasters"), [Double](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Double "Double")-mounts, [Quad](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Quad "Quad")-mounts, [Cannon Enhancements](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cannon_Enhancements "Cannon Enhancements"), and [Fire-Linked](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fire-Linked "Fire-Linked") systems, but there is nothing for [Weapon Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Batteries "Weapon Batteries") at all.

**A:** Just figure out how many weapons you want in your [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") and then add that many [Weapon Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Systems "Weapon Systems"). For example, if you wanted a [Turbolaser](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolaser "Turbolaser") Battery with 6 [Gunners](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gunners "Gunners") (granting a +10 [Aid Another](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Aid_Another "Aid Another") bonus), you would need to add 6 individual [Turbolasers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolasers "Turbolasers"). Than you just group them together and call them a single [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") system.

Alternately, if you are doing the strip-out-and-replace thing, you could probably get away with just doing a 1-for-1 swap of the [Weapon Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Systems "Weapon Systems") (not worrying about splitting [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries") or combining into [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries")), but multiplying costs by the number of component and just giving the [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery") benefits to the new system.

**Q176:** When making a [Starship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship "Starship") from scratch using the [Stock Starships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stock_Starships "Stock Starships") found on page 53 of *[Starships of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships_of_the_Galaxy "Starships of the Galaxy")*, is it even possible to get extra [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") by reducing a ship's starting values when it has less than 10 starting [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") to begin with?

**A:** Yes. In this instance, you round up and simply follow the order of operations. If you worsen a stock ship's starting value in an area by 50%, you gain [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") equal to 2 x 10% of the stock [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points"). For a ship that starts with 5 [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points"), you start with 7 [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") (10% of 5 is .5, rounded up to 1, x 2 is +2, for a total of 7) instead. You can, of course, use the rules under "Gaining [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points")" on page 38 to get more, as well.

**Q177:** Can more than two ships use the **[Synchronized Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Synchronized_Fire "Synchronized Fire")** Talent to gang up on a hapless [Capital Ship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ship "Capital Ship") for massive amounts of damage?

**A:** No. **[Synchronized Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Synchronized_Fire "Synchronized Fire")** only works with paired ships. Other ships may use this Talent as well, but they must pair up. You can't have 6 ships making a single attack this way, but you could have 3 sets of 2 each making an attack.

**Q178:** I can adjust a ship's [Strength](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Strength "Strength"), [Speed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speed "Speed"), and other base traits down 25 or 50% to gain more [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points"), right?

**A:** Wrong. The [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") text on page 53 is referring exclusively to the values on Table 3-9. You cannot lower any of the base stats found in table 3-8 on the previous page in the manner described. Notice the text specifically refers to "values." Table 3-9 is specifically labeled "Stock Ship Base Values" whereas Table 3-8 is not for this very reason.

**Q179:** Can a Colossal ship really equip [Turbolasers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolasers "Turbolasers")? Wouldn't such an upgrade make the ship practically unbeatable in combat when paired with [Combat Thrusters](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Combat_Thrusters "Combat Thrusters")?

**A:** Yes, such a ship would be extremely powerful and unbalanced. No, you cannot equip [Turbolasers](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Turbolasers "Turbolasers") on a Colossal-sized ship. In fact the footnote at the bottom of *Table 3-5: Weapon Systems* in the new *[Starships of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships_of_the_Galaxy "Starships of the Galaxy")* book should refer to Colossal (Frigate) or larger ships, not Colossal. This is official errata.

**Q180:** Can you use the *[Snap Roll](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Snap_Roll "Snap Roll")* Starship Maneuver and the [Vehicular Combat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicular_Combat "Vehicular Combat") Feat against the same attack? Or do you have to pick one or the other?

**A:** You have to pick one or the other against any individual attack.

**Q181:** Does the extra 20 SR from the *[Attack Formation Zeta Nine](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attack_Formation_Zeta_Nine "Attack Formation Zeta Nine")* get lost first or last (like temporary [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") from D&D)? For instance, say I had SR 20 and raised to SR 40 with the [Starship Maneuver](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Maneuver "Starship Maneuver"). I take enough hits to knock my SR down 20 points during a battle. After the battle I end the maneuver. Do I still have SR 20 left over? Or is it SR 0?

**A:** If you have any SR left when you end the maneuver, you lose 20 SR (to a minimum of 0).

**Q182:** How exactly does taking damage to your shields work when you are using the *[Angle Deflector Shields](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Angle_Deflector_Shields "Angle Deflector Shields")* maneuver?

**A:** Your shields are considered doubled/halved with the maneuver, but you don't actually double/halve them.

- So, I have SR 20. I use this, target the boss.
- The boss shoots me for 35. I consider my SR doubled (40) so the shields absorb all the damage and there is no reduction in shields.
- The mook shoots me for 35. I consider my SR halved (10) against him so the SR absorbs 10 and my SR is reduced from 20 to 15.
- The boss shoots me for 35. I still consider my SR doubled (30), so the shields absorb 30 points of damage and my SR is reduced from 15 to 10.

**Q183:** Can you use [Serenity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Serenity "Serenity") to get an automatic Natural 20 when using a [Vehicle Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapon "Vehicle Weapon")? If so, doesn't this make it devastatingly good?

**A:** This isn't clear in the text, but the answer is no.

[Serenity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Serenity "Serenity") says that "your first attack roll... made in the following round is considered to be a Natural 20." This use of "your" was intentional, an artifact left over from earlier phrasing that made this point more clear: any reference to "your attack roll" means that it applies only to a personal, hand-held weapon, but a reference to "an attack" (or attack roll) is not limited this way.

As described on page 144 of the *[Core Rulebook](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Core_Rulebook "Core Rulebook")*, "Your attack roll with a ranged weapon is 1d20 + [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus") + [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") modifier + [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") penalty (if any)." However, on page 167, it says that "An attack roll with a [Vehicle Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapon "Vehicle Weapon") is... 1d20 + [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus") + Vehicle's [Intelligence](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intelligence "Intelligence") modifier + [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") modifier." This difference in phrasing was intentional and meant to show that two different processes were occurring. Since an attack with a [Vehicle Weapon](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapon "Vehicle Weapon") is never made as "1d20 + [Base Attack Bonus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Base_Attack_Bonus "Base Attack Bonus") + [Dexterity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Dexterity "Dexterity") modifier + [Range](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Range "Range") penalty," it is never technically "your attack roll" (first or otherwise), and thus it can't benefit from [Serenity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Serenity "Serenity"). (The Force Power *[Battle Strike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Strike "Battle Strike")* is another example of this.)

Originally, this was part of the justification for why Luke had to turn off his targeting computer to use [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force") and blow up [The Death Star](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Death_Star "The Death Star"). With the targeting computer off, the [Proton Torpedoes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Proton_Torpedoes "Proton Torpedoes") basically became [Improvised](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Improvised "Improvised") ranged weapons, and he was limited to a very short-range shot (within the same [Starship Scale](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Scale "Starship Scale") square) due to the lack of computer assistance. However, Luke could use his personal [Ranged Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ranged_Attack "Ranged Attack") bonus and thus qualify for using a [Destiny Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Destiny_Point "Destiny Point") (or even *[Battle Strike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Strike "Battle Strike")*, which we originally considered making his first [Force Power](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Power "Force Power")).

Obviously, this explicit distinction was lost while condensing everything to fit in the available page count, but the remnants of it ("your attack roll") are still there.

**Q184:** What player in their right mind would make a [Starfighter](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starfighter "Starfighter") with the LIGHT FIGHTER template when they can just use the INTERCEPTOR template, remove the 6-square speed [Sublight Drive](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sublight_Drive "Sublight Drive"), replace them with 5-square speed [Sublight Drive](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sublight_Drive "Sublight Drive"), resulting in a ship that is equal or better in every single way and is cheaper to boot?

**A:** The mistake you seem to be making is selling off the 6-square engine and getting back the full 100,000 credits (effectively making your interceptor worth nothing). You can't do that, not even when making your ship from scratch. Your proposed 5-square speed interceptor would have a base cost of 95,000 credits (100,000 - 25,000 for removing the 6-square [Sublight Drive](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sublight_Drive "Sublight Drive") + 20,000 for adding the 5-square [Sublight Drive](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sublight_Drive "Sublight Drive")). Even when designing a ship's blueprints from nothing, you must still sell components for 1/4 their value (as shown in the [Starship Modifications](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship_Modifications "Starship Modifications") chapter). It may not make much sense, but it is an abstraction to help maintain game balance and to cover any additional costs during ship building that would be too detailed for a game (in other words, you may not literally be selling an engine which you never had, but you must still crunch the numbers as though you did).

**Q185:** Could you please clarify how [Focused Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Focused_Fire "Focused Fire") (from *[Starships of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships_of_the_Galaxy "Starships of the Galaxy")*) is supposed to work?

When it says a single system fires, does it mean a single weapon? Or a single [Weapon Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Battery "Weapon Battery")? Does it take the -5 penalty common with [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attacks? If it fires a [Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battery "Battery"), does it still get the bonus to attacks or damage as [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries") typically do?

**A:** You select one [Weapon System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_System "Weapon System"). Not a [Weapon Battery](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_Battery "Weapon Battery"), but a [Weapon System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Weapon_System "Weapon System"). So, subtract the bonuses built-in from [Gunners](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gunners "Gunners") in [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries"), and it does not get the bonus to damage from [Batteries](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Batteries "Batteries") (as beating a [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense") of 10 would otherwise be ludicrously easy with [Capital Ship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Capital_Ship "Capital Ship") weapons, and would rack up huge damage bonuses). The book does not say that the ship takes a -5 penalty to the attack roll; make no mistake, it is an [Area Attack](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Area_Attack "Area Attack"), but a -5 penalty is NOT associated with all [Area Attacks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Area_Attacks "Area Attacks"), but rather with [Autofire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Autofire "Autofire") attacks. Remember, though, that [Focused Fire](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Focused_Fire "Focused Fire") is an option used by the Gamemaster to speed up gameplay and create engaging [Starship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship "Starship") encounters, and wasn't designed to be some kind of superior [Starship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starship "Starship") combat tactic.

**Q186:** I know I can take cargo space and convert it into extra [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points"), and I know I can take [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") gleaned from the methods shown on table 3-9 of *[Starships of the Galaxy](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Starships_of_the_Galaxy "Starships of the Galaxy")* and convert them back into cargo space, but can I convert ANY extra [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") I have (regardless of its source) and convert it into cargo?

**A:** Yes you can. The footnote says, "Any [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") gained in this way may be kept as spares or used to purchase additional cargo space, adding tons of cargo capacity equal to the Starship's [Cost Modifier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cost_Modifier "Cost Modifier") for each [Emplacement Point](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Point "Emplacement Point") spent."

The "in this way" part does not explicitly state what "this way" is. Is "this way" the changing of stats on a [Stock Starship](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stock_Starship "Stock Starship")? Or is "this way" more specific, referring only to increasing your crew requirements, which is what the previous sentence was talking about? Or is "this way" more general, referring to any change that gives you extra [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points")?

It's the last one. The phrasing that I had in my turnover was this: "Any **leftover** [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") may be kept as spares..." It didn't specify that it applied that particular method at all, and- as I mentioned- it wasn't meant to. I'd meant to point this out elsewhere in the chapter, and that part of the footnote was supposed to be a reminder of how you can add extra cargo by spending [Emplacement Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Emplacement_Points "Emplacement Points") because this was one time you'd be particularly likely to want to do so.

During his final pass while getting it ready for typesetting, the managing editor rephrased it become he didn't like using "leftover" as an adjective, but he wasn't trying to change the rule. Unfortunately, his phrasing compounded my error because it could very easily be read to mean it applies only to [Stock Starships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stock_Starships "Stock Starships").

In the absence of evidence to the contrary, you'd be right to read it conservatively and assume that it only applies to [Stock Starships](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stock_Starships "Stock Starships") because of where it appears in the chapter. But, now that I have pointed out how it is meant to be read, you do have evidence to the contrary.

**Q187:** Can the [Battlefield Effects](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battlefield_Effects "Battlefield Effects") be abused by activating them for two rounds, and having the [Vehicle Weapons](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Vehicle_Weapons "Vehicle Weapons") fire on the second round? Could you use prepared actions to get around the limitations of Battlefield Effects (effectively getting the fire effects at no penalty)?

**A:** Yes. In the former case, the [Battlefield Effects](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battlefield_Effects "Battlefield Effects") will be up for two rounds, down for a round, up for two rounds, down for a round, etc. You can use prepared actions to get around the limitations, though Rodney suggests ruling against such actions.

**Q188:** Can [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") with [Climbing Claws](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Climbing_Claws "Climbing Claws") climb perfectly smooth vertical surfaces? It would seem [General Grievous](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/General_Grievous "General Grievous") and others were capable of doing so in the Clone Wars cartoons.

**A:** No. In that case, I'd argue that he was creating his own hand holds by clamping into the metal itself, making it a Rough Surface.

**Q189:** Can a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") take levels in [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi")?

**A:** Yes, so long as it isn't the Droid's first level. Even so, a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") may never take the [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") Feat, even when [Multiclassing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Multiclassing "Multiclassing") into the [Jedi](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi "Jedi") class.

**Q190:** Do the system components a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") begins play with count against its [Carrying Capacity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Carrying_Capacity "Carrying Capacity")?

**A:** Stock models of [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") are always assumed to be able to carry their stock equipment unencumbered. If you play a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid"), the systems and accessories you buy with your base 1,000 credits are considered to be covered under this clause. Any other equipment encumbers the [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") as normal, including after-market modifications.

**Q191:** If I buy a group of [B1-Series Battle Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/B1-Series_Battle_Droids "B1-Series Battle Droids") (or any other [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") for that matter), can I get them without their guns in order to save money? Does getting them without their carbines effect the license in any way? Do their guns come with ammo upon purchase? Heck, do the [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") even come with the carbines or is that only shown in the stat block of what they are typically fitted with (and are actually supposed to be a separate purchase altogether)?

**A:** The prices in the book cover the stock model with the stock accessories and weapons loadouts. We do not make any provisions for cost with or without certain components, as they are generally not available for sale in any other package. If a Gamemaster wants to make a special exception, they can, but it would be just that: a special exception.

**Q192:** When [Reprogramming](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reprogramming "Reprogramming") new [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents") into a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid"), do they have to be a member of the class that has the [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent") first? Or can I have a 1st-level Droid [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") with [Bounty Hunter Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bounty_Hunter_Talents "Bounty Hunter Talents")?

**A:** [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents") can only be programmed in for the appropriate class levels. If I have a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") with 3 levels of [Soldier](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier "Soldier") and 3 levels of [Scout](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scout "Scout"), I can still only [Reprogram](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reprogram "Reprogram") it with 2 [Soldier Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Soldier_Talents "Soldier Talents") and 2 [Scout Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Scout_Talents "Scout Talents"). [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills"), however, follow no such requirement.

**Q193:** Should your **[Minion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Minion "Minion")** die (see the **[Attract Minion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Attract_Minion "Attract Minion")** Talent of the Crime Lord's [Mastermind Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mastermind_Talent_Tree "Mastermind Talent Tree")), is it replaced with a new one? Or do you lose the benefit of the Talent forever (short of taking it again)?

**A:** Your **[Minion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Minion "Minion")** is not replaced for free. You must spend a [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent") to do so. That is why [Crime Lords](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Crime_Lords "Crime Lords") get so many [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents").

**Q194:** The [Force Disciple](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Disciple "Force Disciple") is able to select talents from "*...any [Force Talent Tree](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Talent_Tree "Force Talent Tree") in Chapter Six: [The Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/The_Force "The Force").*" (p.215) (There is similar wording for the [Jedi Master](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jedi_Master "Jedi Master") and [Sith Lord](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sith_Lord "Sith Lord") Prestige Class.) Does this include the [Force Tradition Talent Trees](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Tradition_Talent_Trees "Force Tradition Talent Trees") in Chapter Six (Jensaarai and Dathomiri Witch) or is it only referring to the Force-sensitive trees (Alter, Control, Dark Side, Sense)? If it's only the Force-sensitive trees, then is that language redundant since someone with [Force Sensitivity](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Sensitivity "Force Sensitivity") can always select [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents") from those trees, or are there times when such a character is allowed to select a [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent") but is not allowed to select from the Force-sensitive trees?

**A:** It includes the [Force Tradition Talent Trees](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Tradition_Talent_Trees "Force Tradition Talent Trees").

**Q197:** Can [Crime Lords](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Crime_Lords "Crime Lords") use their [Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents") to gain [Force Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Talents "Force Talents")?

**A:** Yes, provided they meet the prerequisites.

**Q198:** [Tough as Durasteel](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tough_as_Durasteel "Tough as Durasteel") is listed as a [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"), though it looks like it is meant to be a class feature. Which is it?

**A:** [Tough as Durasteel](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tough_as_Durasteel "Tough as Durasteel") is meant to be a class ability, not a [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"). You gain a cumulative +2 bonus at every even level (which is effectively equal to your Class Level).

### Official Optional Rules

- [Assault](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Assault "Assault") Feat (E2)
- [Maiming Foes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Maiming_Foes "Maiming Foes") (E18)
- [Mechanics Untrained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mechanics_Untrained "Mechanics Untrained") (E20)
- [Sniper Blaster Rifle](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Sniper_Blaster_Rifle "Sniper Blaster Rifle") (E27)
- [Subrepeating Blaster](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Subrepeating_Blaster "Subrepeating Blaster") (E27)

## Saga Edition Conversion Guide

*By Gary M. Sarli*

Ultimately, the best way to start a campaign for the new *Saga Edition* of the *Star Wars Roleplaying Game* is by generating new characters. However, many players will want to transfer characters from the previous version of the rules outlined in the *Revised Core Rulebook*. These guidelines are designed to help you convert characters, equipment, and other mechanics from the previous version into the new rules.

Throughout the process, remember that conversion from one game system to another is by definition an inexact science. Though the guidelines provide a foundation for conversion, they can't be exhaustive, nor are they perfect. You'll see a lot of vague references to approximation, estimates, and "Starting Points." You and your Gamemaster should work together to ensure that the spirit of your character is preserved during the process, even if the numbers don't always add up the same.

The BasicsSpeciesDroidsClasses and Prestige ClassesSkillsFeatsEquipmentVehicles and StarshipsCreatures

There have been a few simple changes to basic mechanics that apply to virtually every character in the game.

**Hit Points:** For Heroic Characters, [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") are equal to Vitality Points, but add twice your maximum Vitality Points from your class at 1st level to your total. For example, a Soldier 5/Noble 3 would add 20 [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") to their total; their class at first level has 1d10 Vitality Points, and twice the maximum (10 Vitality Points) yields +20 [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points").

For [Nonheroic](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Nonheroic "Nonheroic") characters, [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") are 1d4 + [Constitution](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Constitution "Constitution") modifier per level (an average of 2.5 [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") + [Constitution](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Constitution "Constitution") modifier per level, rounded down).

**Defenses:** Your [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense"), [Fortitude Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude_Defense "Fortitude Defense"), and [Will Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Will_Defense "Will Defense") scores are calculated with a new method. Each [Defense Score](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Defense_Score "Defense Score") has a value of 10 + Heroic Level (or Armor bonus, if any) + relevant [Ability](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability "Ability") modifier + best class defense bonus + size modifier (if any). See [Heroic Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heroic_Classes "Heroic Classes") for a list of class defense bonuses, and see each [Prestige Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prestige_Classes "Prestige Classes") for their individual class defense bonuses.

**Damage Threshold:** Your [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") is equal to your [Fortitude Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude_Defense "Fortitude Defense") unless you are greater than Medium size (see "Creatures") or you have a [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"), [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat"), or class feature that increases it.

For the most part, [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") from the previous rules are playable in the new rules. The primary changes you'll need to make are outlined below. As always, compare the new Species to existing [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") in *Saga Edition* to see if your conversion is significantly better or worse than those [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species"), making adjustments as you see fit. Species that originally appeared in the *Revised Core Rulebook* but not in the *Saga Edition [Core Rulebook](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Core_Rulebook "Core Rulebook")* are outlined in the table below. For conversions, follow the guidelines below.

| SPECIES | ABILITY MODIFIERS | IMAGE |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Geonosian](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Geonosian "Geonosian") | +2 Str, -2 Int, -2 Wis, -2 Cha |  |
| [Jawa](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jawa "Jawa") | +2 Dex, -2 Str, -2 Cha |  |
| [Kaminoan](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Kaminoan "Kaminoan") | +2 Con, -2 Wis |  |
| [Noghri](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noghri "Noghri") | +2 Dex, -4 Cha |  |
| [Tusken Raider](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Tusken_Raider "Tusken Raider") | +2 Con, -2 Int, -2 Wis |  |

## Speed

Your new [Speed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speed "Speed") (in 1.5-meter squares) is determined by your old Speed (in meters), as shown in the chart below.

| OLD SPEED | NEW SPEED |
| --- | --- |
| 18-20 Meters | 10 Squares |
| 14-16 Meters | 8 Squares |
| 10-12 Meters | 6 Squares |
| 6-8 Meters | 4 Squares |
| 4 Meters | 2 Squares |
| 2 Meters | 1 Square |

If you have an additional movement type (for example, a Climb Speed or a Swim Speed), convert the speed to squares as described above. Any [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") with a Climb Speed can reroll [Climb](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Climb "Climb") checks or [Take 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Take_10 "Take 10") even under stress, and any [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") with a Swim Speed can reroll [Swim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swim "Swim") checks or [Take 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Take_10 "Take 10") even under stress.

## Skills

If the Species gains a skill bonus of some sort, replace it with one of the options outlined below; of these, the "reroll" option is by far the most common. Generally, a Species shouldn't have special rules for more than two [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills").

**Reroll:** You may reroll a skill check for a given [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill"), but you must keep the second result. If the Species' bonus in the previous rules only applied to part of a [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill") (for example, [Intimidation](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intimidation "Intimidation") is now just a part of [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion")), limit the reroll to a single use of that [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill") (for example, you can reroll only when using [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") to [intimidate](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Intimidate "Intimidate")). This is best used for a Species that is naturally good- or, rather, naturally unlikely to fail- with a given [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill"), whether [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") or not.

**Conditional Bonus Feat:** You gain [Skill Focus](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Focus "Skill Focus") if you are [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") in that [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill"). This is best for [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills") where the Species is best known for producing highly skilled experts or professionals in a given field. For example, [Bothans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Bothans "Bothans") make exceptionally good spies, but they spend a lot of time training to achieve that distinction. In addition, this may be used as a way to further enhance a Species' natural aptitude with a [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill") that already gets a reroll. For example, [Cereans](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Cereans "Cereans") get a reroll and a Conditional Bonus Feat for [Initiative](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Initiative "Initiative").

**Take 10:** You may [Take 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Take_10 "Take 10") at any time with the [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill"). This is usually reserved for [Swim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swim "Swim") and [Climb](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Climb "Climb") checks when the Species has a Swim or Climb Speed, respectively.

## Other Abilities

Most other abilities should have a clear analog. For example, a Species that used to gain Iron Will as a Bonus Feat should instead get a +2 [Species](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Species "Species") bonus to its [Will Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Will_Defense "Will Defense"). Similarly, a Species with Toughness as a Bonus Feat should gain either [Toughness](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Toughness "Toughness") (if you want them to last a bit longer in a fight) or [Improved Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Improved_Damage_Threshold "Improved Damage Threshold") (if you want them to be hard to hurt or kill). [Low-Light Vision](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Low-Light_Vision "Low-Light Vision") and [Darkvision](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Darkvision "Darkvision") are kept intact, but make sure you use the versions shown in [Visibility](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Visibility "Visibility").

[Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") are the one aspect of the new rules that are best converted by starting from scratch. Unlike the previous rules, *Saga Edition* presents a comprehensive system for designing and building new [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids"), so retaining the balance in the new rules can be tricky. Still, if you want to convert a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") and keep it as close as possible to the previous version, follow the steps outlined below.

**Abilities:** For unique, Heroic [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids") (such as one created specifically for a player character), no adjustment is necessary. For mass-produced models of [Droids](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droids "Droids"), determine the Droid's natural [Ability Scores](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Scores "Ability Scores") by subtracting the size modifiers by degree and size (see [Playing a Custom Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Playing_a_Custom_Droid "Playing a Custom Droid")); discard the Droid's original [Constitution](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Constitution "Constitution") score, since it is not used in the new rules. Replace these [Ability Scores](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Scores "Ability Scores") with 13, 12, 11, 10, and 9, from highest to lowest (or 15, 14, 13, 12, and 10 if the Droid's first level is in a Heroic Class). If two [Ability Scores](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Scores "Ability Scores") have the same value, you may put the higher score in whichever one you wish. Finally, add the degree and size modifiers back into the [Ability Scores](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ability_Scores "Ability Scores") to determine their final value.

**Hit Points and Damage Threshold:** If the [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") is Large or greater size, make sure you add the appropriate adjustment to the Droid's [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") and [Damage Threshold](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Damage_Threshold "Damage Threshold") (see "Droid Size" under [Playing a Custom Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Playing_a_Custom_Droid "Playing a Custom Droid")).

**Speed:** Determine the Droid's [Speed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Speed "Speed") as shown for "Species," up to the maximum listed for the Droid's [Locomotion System](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Locomotion_System "Locomotion System") and size.

**Unarmed Damage:** Replace the Droid's [Unarmed](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Unarmed "Unarmed") damage with the appropriate amount for its size and [Appendages](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Appendages "Appendages").

**Other Systems and Accessories:** Most other [Droid Systems](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid_Systems "Droid Systems") should have a clear equivalent in the new rules. When in doubt, compare to a [Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Droid "Droid") that has game statistics in both systems (such as an [R2-Series Astromech Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/R2-Series_Astromech_Droid "R2-Series Astromech Droid"), a [3PO-Series Protocol Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/3PO-Series_Protocol_Droid "3PO-Series Protocol Droid"), or a [B1-Series Battle Droid](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/B1-Series_Battle_Droid "B1-Series Battle Droid")).

[Heroic Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heroic_Classes "Heroic Classes") and [Prestige Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prestige_Classes "Prestige Classes") are substantially changed from their previous versions. Instead of having predetermined class features that are granted at particular levels, [Heroic Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heroic_Classes "Heroic Classes") and [Prestige Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prestige_Classes "Prestige Classes") in *Saga Edition* have "[Talents](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talents "Talents")" that can potentially be selected in any order. This maximizes the degree of customization possible in the new rules, but it makes converting an old character somewhat tricky.

Keep in mind that some classes have been merged with others, and each class is meant to be very flexible in its scope. Don't get caught up on the name of a class; instead, focus on its functionality and role. For example, an Officer, Business Executive, or Professor might all be members of the [Noble](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Noble "Noble") Heroic Class despite having no aristocratic background. They are logical members of that class because of their leadership skills and education.

## Other Prestige Classes

To convert other Prestige Classes to the *Saga Edition* rules, use existing [Prestige Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Prestige_Classes "Prestige Classes") as a starting point for a comparison. A few specific suggestions are outlined below.

**Requirements:** Convert Skill Rank requirements to being [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") in the equivalent [Skill](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill "Skill"), and add a "Minimum Character Level" requirement equal to (required Skill Ranks) - 3. For other requirements, look for an equivalent [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"), [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat"), or class feature.

**Hit Points:** Prestige Classes should grant the same number of [Hit Points](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Hit_Points "Hit Points") at each level as their old versions granted in Vitality Points.

**Starting Feats:** Prestige Classes should not have Starting Feats in the new rules.

**Defense Bonuses:** To figure out a Prestige Class's class [Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Defense "Defense") bonuses, look at the previous progression of [Fortitude](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude "Fortitude"), [Reflex](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex "Reflex"), and [Will](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Will "Will") saving throws to determine if they are "high" (starting at +2), "medium" (starting at +1), or "low" (starting at +0). Generally, a high bonus should be +4 in the new rules, a medium bonus should be +2, and a low bonus should be +0. For example, a Prestige Class with a good Fortitude save and a medium Reflex save should have class [Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Defense "Defense") bonuses of +4 [Fortitude Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Fortitude_Defense "Fortitude Defense") and +2 [Reflex Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Reflex_Defense "Reflex Defense").

The total of all class [Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Defense "Defense") bonuses should be +6 for most Prestige Classes. If you went over this amount, reduce class [Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Defense "Defense") Bonuses (starting with the lowest) until you reach the right amount. For example, if a class had two high and one medium saving throw, this would be +4, +4, and +2- four points too many. Reducing the lowest class bonus first, we're left with +4 and +4 in the highest two- still two points too many. Removing points from them equally yields +3 and +3- a total of six points.

## Feature Conversions

Most Class Features can be left intact or used with slight adjustments. Note that the new rules generally avoid giving a bonus to [Skill Checks](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Checks "Skill Checks"). Instead, consider abilities that reduce penalties, allow rerolls, or allow the character to [Take 10](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Take_10 "Take 10") even under stress. As always, look for an existing [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent") or class feature in the *Saga Edition* rules that is close to the previous class feature.

Skills have changed substantially in that there are no longer Skill Ranks, and most Skills have been combined into new [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills"). To convert your character's Skills, look at the number of ranks in each Skill. If you have a number of ranks equal to at least half your Character Level, you should probably be [Trained](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained "Trained") in the equivalent Skill (or possess the [Talent](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Talent "Talent"), [Feat](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Feat "Feat"), or other mechanic) shown on the table below.

If you run out of [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") (determined by your starting class), consider taking the [Skill Training](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skill_Training "Skill Training") Feat (available as a Bonus Feat to all [Heroic Classes](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Heroic_Classes "Heroic Classes")). If you have [Trained Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Trained_Skills "Trained Skills") left over, allocate them to whatever [Skills](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Skills "Skills") best fit your character's background and personality.

| OLD SKILL | NEW MECHANIC |  | OLD SKILL | NEW MECHANIC |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Appraise | [Knowledge (Technology)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Technology) "Knowledge (Technology)") | Knowledge (Tactics) | [Knowledge (Tactics)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Tactics) "Knowledge (Tactics)") |
| Astrogate | [Use Computer](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_Computer "Use Computer") | Knowledge (Technology) | [Knowledge (Technology)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Technology) "Knowledge (Technology)") |
| Balance | [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") | Knowledge (Wilderness Lore) | [Knowledge (Life Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Life_Sciences) "Knowledge (Life Sciences)") or [Survival](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Survival "Survival") |
| Bluff | [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception") | Knowledge (World Lore) | [Knowledge (Galactic Lore)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Galactic_Lore) "Knowledge (Galactic Lore)") |
| Climb | [Climb](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Climb "Climb") | Listen | [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception") |
| Computer Use | [Use Computer](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_Computer "Use Computer") | Move Silently | [Stealth](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stealth "Stealth") |
| Craft | [Mechanics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mechanics "Mechanics") or [Knowledge (Technology)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Technology) "Knowledge (Technology)") | Pilot | [Pilot](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Pilot "Pilot") |
| Demolitions | [Mechanics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mechanics "Mechanics") and **[Demolitionist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Demolitionist "Demolitionist")** | Profession | N/A |
| Diplomacy | [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") | Read/Write Language | [Linguist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Linguist "Linguist") |
| Disable Device | [Mechanics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mechanics "Mechanics") | Ride | [Ride](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Ride "Ride") |
| Disguise | [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception") | Search | [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception") |
| Entertain | [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") | Sense Motive | [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception") or [Will Defense](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Will_Defense "Will Defense") |
| Escape Artist | [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") | Sleight of Hand | [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception") |
| Forgery | [Deception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Deception "Deception") | Speak Language | [Linguist](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Linguist "Linguist") |
| Gamble | **[Gambler](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gambler "Gambler")** | Spot | [Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Perception "Perception") |
| Gather Information | [Gather Information](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Gather_Information "Gather Information") | Survival | [Survival](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Survival "Survival") |
| Handle Animal | [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") or [Knowledge (Life Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Life_Sciences) "Knowledge (Life Sciences)") | Swim | [Swim](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Swim "Swim") |
| Hide | [Stealth](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Stealth "Stealth") | Treat Injury | [Treat Injury](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Treat_Injury "Treat Injury") |
| Intimidate | [Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Persuasion "Persuasion") | Tumble | [Acrobatics](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Acrobatics "Acrobatics") |
| Jump | [Jump](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Jump "Jump") | Affect Mind | *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* |
| Knowledge (Alien Species) | [Knowledge (Life Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Life_Sciences) "Knowledge (Life Sciences)") or [Knowledge (Social Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Social_Sciences) "Knowledge (Social Sciences)") | Battlemind | *[Battle Strike](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Battle_Strike "Battle Strike")* |
| Knowledge (Archaeology) | [Knowledge (Galactic Lore)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Galactic_Lore) "Knowledge (Galactic Lore)") or [Knowledge (Social Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Social_Sciences) "Knowledge (Social Sciences)") | Drain Energy | N/A |
| Knowledge (Architecture) | [Knowledge (Technology)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Technology) "Knowledge (Technology)") | Empathy | **[Force Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Perception "Force Perception")** or **[Force Persuasion](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Persuasion "Force Persuasion")** |
| Knowledge (Astronomy) | [Knowledge (Physical Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Physical_Sciences) "Knowledge (Physical Sciences)") | Enhance Ability | N/A |
| Knowledge (Biology) | [Knowledge (Life Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Life_Sciences) "Knowledge (Life Sciences)") | Enhance Senses | [Use the Force](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Use_the_Force "Use the Force") or **[Force Perception](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Perception "Force Perception")** |
| Knowledge (Bureaucracy) | [Knowledge (Bureaucracy)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Bureaucracy) "Knowledge (Bureaucracy)") | Farseeing | *[Farseeing](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Farseeing "Farseeing")* |
| Knowledge (Buisness) | [Knowledge (Bureaucracy)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Bureaucracy) "Knowledge (Bureaucracy)") | Fear | *[Mind Trick](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Mind_Trick "Mind Trick")* |
| Knowledge (Chemistry) | [Knowledge (Physical Sciences)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Physical_Sciences) "Knowledge (Physical Sciences)") | Force Defense | *[Rebuke](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Rebuke "Rebuke")* |
| Knowledge (Engineering) | [Knowledge (Technology)](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Knowledge_(Technology) "Knowledge (Technology)") | Force Grip | *[Force Grip](https://swse.miraheze.org/wiki/Force_Grip "Force Grip")* |
